local function scanError()
    if not Cache.HDSignals then return end
    local children = Cache.HDSignals:GetChildren()
    for i = 1, #children do
        if not isWhitelisted(children[i].Name) and isErrorSignal(children[i].Name) then applyError(children[i]) end
    end
end

local errorConns = {}
local function startAntiError()
    if protectionStates.Error then return end
    protectionStates.Error = true
    scanError()
    table.insert(errorConns, ReplicatedStorage.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if d.Parent and d.Parent.Name == "Signals" and not isWhitelisted(d.Name) and isErrorSignal(d.Name) then
            task.delay(0.3, scanError)
        end
    end))
end

local function stopAntiError()
    protectionStates.Error = false
    for i = 1, #errorConns do pcall(function() errorConns[i]:Disconnect() end) end
    table.clear(errorConns)
end

local function restoreAntiError()
    for c in pairs(errorDisabledConns) do pcall(function() c:Enable() end) end
    table.clear(errorDisabledConns)
    for obj, data in pairs(errorData) do
        pcall(function()
            if not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
            if data.type == "script" then obj.Enabled = data.enabled
            elseif data.type == "remote" then obj.Name = data.name end
        end)
    end
    table.clear(errorData)
end

local warpConns = {}
local function startAntiWarp()
    if protectionStates.Warp then return end
    protectionStates.Warp = true
    table.insert(warpConns, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if not isWhitelisted(d.Name) and isWarpName(d.Name) then task.defer(applyWarp, d) end
    end))
    table.insert(warpConns, Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if not isWhitelisted(d.Name) and isWarpName(d.Name) then task.defer(applyWarp, d) end
    end))
    table.insert(warpConns, ReplicatedStorage.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if not isWhitelisted(d.Name) and (d:IsA("RemoteEvent") or d:IsA("RemoteFunction")) and isWarpName(d.Name) then
            task.defer(applyWarp, d)
        end
    end))
end

local function stopAntiWarp()
    protectionStates.Warp = false
    for i = 1, #warpConns do pcall(function() warpConns[i]:Disconnect() end) end
    table.clear(warpConns)
end

local function restoreAntiWarp()
    for obj, parent in pairs(warpHidden) do
        pcall(function() if obj and parent then obj.Parent = parent end end)
    end
    for obj, name in pairs(warpRenamed) do
        pcall(function() if obj and obj.Parent then obj.Name = name end end)
    end
    table.clear(warpHidden)
    table.clear(warpRenamed)
end

local BLUR_LIMIT = 15
local blurConns = {}
local blurLoopTask = nil

local function killBadBlur(obj)
    if not obj then return end
    if isWhitelisted(obj.Name) then return end
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("BlurEffect") then
            if obj.Size > BLUR_LIMIT then
                obj.Size = 0
                obj.Enabled = false
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        elseif obj:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or obj:IsA("BloomEffect") or obj:IsA("SunRaysEffect") then
            local n = safeLower(obj.Name)
            if string.find(n, "crash", 1, true) or string.find(n, "blind", 1, true) or string.find(n, "trip", 1, true) or string.find(n, "blur", 1, true) then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function startAntiBlur()
    if protectionStates.Blur then return end
    protectionStates.Blur = true
    table.insert(blurConns, Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(killBadBlur))
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam then table.insert(blurConns, cam.ChildAdded:Connect(killBadBlur)) end
    blurLoopTask = task.spawn(function()
        while protectionStates.Blur do
            task.wait(0.25)
            pcall(function()
                local kids = Lighting:GetChildren()
                for i = 1, #kids do killBadBlur(kids[i]) end
                local c = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if c then
                    local camKids = c:GetChildren()
                    for i = 1, #camKids do killBadBlur(camKids[i]) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAntiBlur()
    protectionStates.Blur = false
    for i = 1, #blurConns do pcall(function() blurConns[i]:Disconnect() end) end
    table.clear(blurConns)
    if blurLoopTask then task.cancel(blurLoopTask); blurLoopTask = nil end
end

local function restoreAntiBlur() end

local CrashUIMustProtect = {
    "chat","bubble","textchat","textchatservice",
    "backpack","robux","leaderboard","topbar",
    "health","moveset","skill","ability",
    "shop","store","inventory","craft",
    "quest","dialog","hud","minimap",
    "tro","wavor","vex","dark","monster","black","alfa","fgr",
    "hdadmin","notices","commandmenu","rank","hr","mr","cmds","cmdlist"
}

local function isCrashProtectedUI(name)
    if not name or name == "" then return false end
    if isWhitelisted(name) then return true end
    local l = safeLower(name)
    for i = 1, #CrashUIMustProtect do
        if string.find(l, CrashUIMustProtect[i], 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local crashExisting = {}
local function snapshotCrash()
    table.clear(crashExisting)
    pcall(function() for _, o in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do crashExisting[o] = true end end)
    pcall(function() for _, o in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do crashExisting[o] = true end end)
    pcall(function() for _, o in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do crashExisting[o] = true end end)
end

local CrashBadWords = {
    "crash","crasher","kaboom","nuke","rapture",
    "lag","spam","ddos","exploit","molotov",
    "freeze","frozen","blind","trip",
    "destroy","killall","banall","kickall"
}

local function isCrashBadName(name)
    if not name or name == "" then return false end
    if isWhitelisted(name) then return false end
    local l = safeLower(name)
    for i = 1, #CrashBadWords do
        if string.find(l, CrashBadWords[i], 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function crashNukeIfNew(obj)
    if not obj or not protectionStates.Crash then return end
    if crashExisting[obj] then return end
    if isCrashProtectedUI(obj.Name) then return end
    if not isCrashBadName(obj.Name) then return end
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function()
            if not obj.Parent then return end
            if obj:IsA("Script") or obj:IsA("LocalScript") or obj:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                obj.Enabled = false
            end
            if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                pcall(function() obj.OnClientEvent:Connect(function() return end) end)
            end
            obj:Destroy()
        end)
    end)
end

local function crashHookUniversal()
    table.insert(crashConns, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(crashNukeIfNew))
    table.insert(crashConns, playerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(crashNukeIfNew))
    table.insert(crashConns, Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(crashNukeIfNew))
    table.insert(crashConns, ReplicatedStorage.DescendantAdded:Connect(crashNukeIfNew))
    pcall(function() table.insert(crashConns, CoreGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(crashNukeIfNew)) end)
end

local function crashFastScan()
    workspaceTask = task.spawn(function()
        while protectionStates.Crash do
            task.wait(0.5)
            pcall(function()
                for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                    if not crashExisting[c] and not isCrashProtectedUI(c.Name) and isCrashBadName(c.Name) then
                        pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                for _, c in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
                    if not crashExisting[c] and not isCrashProtectedUI(c.Name) and isCrashBadName(c.Name) then
                        pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                for _, c in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if not crashExisting[c] and isCrashBadName(c.Name) then
                        pcall(function() c:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function backupCrashUIs()
    table.clear(uiBackups)
    pcall(function()
        for _, g in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
            if isCrashProtectedUI(g.Name) then
                pcall(function() uiBackups[g.Name] = g:Clone() end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function crashHookUIDelete()
    table.insert(crashConns, playerGui.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
        if not protectionStates.Crash then return end
        task.wait(0.1)
        pcall(function()
            if uiBackups[child.Name] and not playerGui:FindFirstChild(child.Name) then
                local clone = uiBackups[child.Name]:Clone()
                clone.Name = child.Name
                clone.Parent = playerGui
            end
        end)
    end))
    table.insert(crashConns, playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if not protectionStates.Crash then return end
        if isCrashProtectedUI(child.Name) and not uiBackups[child.Name] then
            task.wait(0.3)
            pcall(function() uiBackups[child.Name] = child:Clone() end)
        end
    end))
    uiRestoreTask = task.spawn(function()
        while protectionStates.Crash do
            task.wait(1)
            pcall(function()
                for name, backup in pairs(uiBackups) do
                    if not playerGui:FindFirstChild(name) then
                        local clone = backup:Clone()
                        clone.Name = name
                        clone.Parent = playerGui
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function crashBlockKick(obj)
    if not obj or not obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return end
    if kickBlocked[obj] then return end
    local l = safeLower(obj.Name)
    if string.find(l, "kick", 1, true) or string.find(l, "ban", 1, true) or string.find(l, "crash", 1, true) or string.find(l, "expel", 1, true) then
        kickBlocked[obj] = true
        pcall(function() obj.OnClientEvent:Connect(function() return end) end)
    end
end

local function crashHookKick()
    pcall(function()
        local desc = ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #desc do crashBlockKick(desc[i]) end
    end)
    table.insert(crashConns, ReplicatedStorage.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not protectionStates.Crash then return end
        task.defer(crashBlockKick, obj)
    end))
    kickScanTask = task.spawn(function()
        while protectionStates.Crash do
            task.wait(2)
            pcall(function()
                local desc = ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()
                for i = 1, #desc do
                    if desc[i]:IsA("RemoteEvent") then crashBlockKick(desc[i]) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function crashAntiFreeze()
    antiFreezeTask = task.spawn(function()
        while protectionStates.Crash do
            task.wait(0.08)
            pcall(function()
                local char = player.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hum then
                    if hum.WalkSpeed <= 0 or hum.WalkSpeed > 100 then hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
                    if hum.UseJumpPower and (hum.JumpPower <= 0 or hum.JumpPower > 200) then hum.JumpPower = 50 end
                    if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end
                    if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
                end
                if root and root.Anchored then root.Anchored = false end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function crashExplosionCleanup()
    explosionTask = task.spawn(function()
        while protectionStates.Crash do
            task.wait(0.2)
            pcall(function()
                local char = player.Character
                if not char then return end
                local kids = char:GetChildren()
                for i = 1, #kids do
                    local k = kids[i]
                    if k:IsA("Explosion") or k:IsA("Fire") or k:IsA("Smoke") or k:IsA("Sparkles") then
                        pcall(function() k:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function crashAnimCleaner()
    if animCleanConn then return end
    local fc = 0
    animCleanConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        fc = fc + 1
        if fc < 10 then return end
        fc = 0
        pcall(function()
            local char = player.Character
            if char then
                local kids = char:GetDescendants()
                for i = 1, #kids do
                    if kids[i]:IsA("Animation") and isCrashBadName(kids[i].Name) then
                        pcall(function() kids[i]:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
end

local function stopCrashAnimCleaner()
    if animCleanConn then
        animCleanConn:Disconnect()
        animCleanConn = nil
    end
end

local function startAntiCrash()
    if protectionStates.Crash then return end
    protectionStates.Crash = true
    snapshotCrash()
    pcall(backupCrashUIs)
    pcall(crashHookUniversal)
    pcall(crashFastScan)
    pcall(crashHookUIDelete)
    pcall(crashHookKick)
    pcall(crashAntiFreeze)
    pcall(crashExplosionCleanup)
    pcall(crashAnimCleaner)
    task.wait(0.4)
end

local function stopAntiCrash()
    protectionStates.Crash = false
    for i = 1, #crashConns do pcall(function() crashConns[i]:Disconnect() end) end
    table.clear(crashConns)
    if antiFreezeTask then task.cancel(antiFreezeTask); antiFreezeTask = nil end
    if uiRestoreTask then task.cancel(uiRestoreTask); uiRestoreTask = nil end
    if kickScanTask then task.cancel(kickScanTask); kickScanTask = nil end
    if explosionTask then task.cancel(explosionTask); explosionTask = nil end
    if workspaceTask then task.cancel(workspaceTask); workspaceTask = nil end
    pcall(stopCrashAnimCleaner)
    table.clear(uiBackups)
    table.clear(kickBlocked)
    table.clear(crashExisting)
end

local function startAllProtections()
    pcall(startAntiNV)
    pcall(startAntiAlerts)
    pcall(startBlockCommand)
    pcall(startECC)
    pcall(startRemoteNuke)
    pcall(startRR)
    pcall(startAntiError)
    pcall(startAntiWarp)
    pcall(startAntiBlur)
end

local function stopAllProtections()
    pcall(stopAntiNV); pcall(restoreAntiNV)
    pcall(stopAntiAlerts); pcall(restoreAntiAlerts)
    pcall(stopBlockCommand); pcall(restoreBlockCommand)
    pcall(stopECC); pcall(restoreECC)
    pcall(stopRemoteNuke); pcall(restoreRemoteNuke)
    pcall(stopRR); pcall(restoreRR)
    pcall(stopAntiError); pcall(restoreAntiError)
    pcall(stopAntiWarp); pcall(restoreAntiWarp)
    pcall(stopAntiBlur); pcall(restoreAntiBlur)
end

local function refreshIndicators()
    for key, dot in pairs(indLabels) do
        local on = protectionStates[key]
        if lastState[key] ~= on then
            lastState[key] = on
            pcall(function()
                dot.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(200, 40, 40)
            end)
        end
    end
end

    refreshIndicators()
    if isProtectionActive then
        statusLabel.Text = "● FIREWALL: SECURED | ROOT ACTIVE"

        protectBtn.Text = "⏹️ ABORT SECURITY PROTOCOL"
        protectBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 18, 45)
        btnStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 110, 255)
    else
        statusLabel.Text = "● FIREWALL: VULNERABLE"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
        protectBtn.Text = "🛡 INITIATE SECURITY GRID"
        protectBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 35, 80)
        btnStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(20, 65, 140)
    end
    
    if isCrashActive then
        crashBtn.Text = "💀 ANTI-CRASH [ON]"
        crashBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 10, 15)
        crashBtnStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 80)
    else
        crashBtn.Text = "💀 ANTI-CRASH [OFF]"
        crashBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(2, 12, 30)
        crashBtnStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 20, 40)
    end
end

local function startContinuousProgressBar()
    task.spawn(function()
        while isProtectionActive do
            local t1 = TweenService:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            t1:Play()
            t1.Completed:Wait()
            if not isProtectionActive then break end
            local t2 = TweenService:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
            t2:Play()
            t2.Completed:Wait()
        end
        TweenService:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    end)
end

local function startTerminalLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while isProtectionActive do
            local ops = {
                "> [DECRYPT] Solving Discrete Logarithm: g^x = A (mod p)...",
                "> [DEFENSE] Tracking intrusion pattern... Rootkit trace blocked.",
                "> [SHIELD] Firewall rules compiled: Block incoming on Ports [22, 80, 443]",
                "> [INTEGRITY] Scanning Memory Pages validated.",
                "> [CIPHER] Initiating AES-256-GCM authentication block cipher...",
                "> [ANTI-TAMPER] Hooking debug registers DR0-DR3. Obfuscation level: MAX",
                "> [MATRIX] Transforming coordinates: Det(M) = " .. string.format("%.4f", math.random() * 10),
                "> [CYBER-WAR] Rerouting brute-force vectors to Virtual Honeypot",
                "> [CLEANSE] Memory garbage collector flushed."
            }
            local phrase = ops[math.random(1, #ops)]
            terminalBox.Text = phrase .. "\n" .. terminalBox.Text
            local lines = string.split(terminalBox.Text, "\n")
            if #lines > 5 then
                table.remove(lines, #lines)
                terminalBox.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            end
            task.wait(math.random(4, 8) / 10) 
        end
    end)
end

local function startGlitchLoop()
    local nodesSecured = math.random(50000, 75000)
    task.spawn(function()
        while isProtectionActive do
            nodesSecured = nodesSecured + math.random(24, 180)
            local randomMac = string.format("02:00:00:%02X:%02X:%02X", math.random(0,255), math.random(0,255), math.random(0,255))
            interceptLabel.Text = "SECURED: " .. nodesSecured .. " | MAC: " .. randomMac
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
end

local dragging, dragStart, startPos
icon.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = iconContainer.Position
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        iconContainer.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = false
    end
end)

    {
        name = 'مصفوفة التخفي',
        icon = '👻',
        title = 'TRO + WAVOR — منشأة التخفي',
        source = [=[--[[
    TRO STEALTH MATRIX // OVERLORD FACILITY v12.4
    [ SECURE PROXY STRUCTURE INCLUDED FOR MOONVEIL ]
]]

-- ========================================================
-- 1. المحفظة الوهمية (Secure Proxy) - MoonVeil سيعقد هذا الجزء جداً
-- ========================================================
local SecureProxy = setmetatable({}, {
    __index = function(self, key)
        if key == "GetS" then
            return function(serviceName) 
                return game:GetService(serviceName) 
            end
        elseif key == "Me" then
            return game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        elseif key == "HideUI" then
            return function(guiElement)
                -- محاولة استخدام gethui للإخفاء التام
                local success, hiddenUI = pcall(function() return gethui() end)
                if success and hiddenUI then
                    guiElement.Parent = hiddenUI
                else
                    -- الخيار البديل الآمن
                    local cgSuccess, cg = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
                    if cgSuccess and cg then
                        guiElement.Parent = cg
                    else
                        guiElement.Parent = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif key == "Honeypot" then
            -- فخ لمن يحاول فحص السكربت
            return "TRO_AUTH_BYPASS_9921_IGNORE"
        end
    end,
    __newindex = function(self, key, value)
        error("Matrix Violation Detected.")
    end
})

-- تفعيل الفخ
local _fakeAuth = SecureProxy.Honeypot

    Bg = Color3.fromRGB(15, 8, 12),

    Secondary = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0),

    SubText = Color3.fromRGB(180, 150, 160),

}

    HideSelf = false,
    HideAll = false,
    SpecificPlayers = {},
    DataSuppressor = false,
    ChatSuppressed = {},
    HiddenChatElements = {},

    IsOpen = false,
    HiddenPlayers = {}
}

local function CreateGUI()
    -- تنظيف الواجهات القديمة بأمان

        local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

        Txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        Txt.TextSize = 12

        Txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        Txt.Parent = Frame

        Sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 15)
        Sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 24)
        Sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        Btn.Text = ""
        Btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        Btn.Parent = Frame
        Instance.new("UICorner", Btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local Circle = Instance.new("Frame")
        Circle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        Circle.Position = defaultVal and UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
        Circle.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
        Circle.Parent = Btn
        Instance.new("UICorner", Circle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local active = defaultVal
        Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            active = not active
            callback(active)
            TweenService:Create(Btn, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {BackgroundColor3 = active and Theme.Primary or Theme.Bg}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(Circle, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Position = active and UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)}):Play()
        end)
    end

    CreateModule("SELF STEALTH MODE", "إخفاء شخصيتك واستعادة مظهرك فوراً", false, function(s)
        State.HideSelf = s
        pcall(function() ApplyPlayerState(LocalPlayer) end)
        LogTerminal(s and "SELF STEALTH: ENABLED" or "SELF STEALTH: DISABLED", s and Theme.Secondary or Theme.Red)

        State.HideAll = s
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            pcall(function() ApplyPlayerState(p) end)
        end
        LogTerminal(s and "GLOBAL PURGE: ACTIVE" or "GLOBAL PURGE: DEACTIVATED", s and Theme.Secondary or Theme.Red)
    end)

    CreateModule("KINETIC EXPLOSION SHIELD", "حظر تأثيرات الانفجارات البصرية", true, function(s)
        State.AntiExplosion = s

    end)

    CreateModule("DATA SERVICE SUPPRESSOR", "إخفاء كلام ومؤشرات اللاعبين المخفيين", false, function(s)
        State.DataSuppressor = s
        pcall(function() ApplyDataSuppressorToAll() end)
        LogTerminal(s and "DATA SUPPRESSOR: ARMED" or "DATA SUPPRESSOR: DISARMED", s and Theme.Green or Theme.Red)
    end)

    local TxtTarget = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    TxtTarget.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
    TxtTarget.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TxtTarget.Text = "SPECIFIC TARGET MATRIX:"
    TxtTarget.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    TxtTarget.TextSize = 11
    TxtTarget.TextColor3 = Theme.Primary
    TxtTarget.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    TxtTarget.Parent = Container

    local Scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    Scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -190)
    Scroll.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
    Scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 2
    Scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Primary
    Scroll.Parent = Container
    Instance.new("UICorner", Scroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    local ScrollLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    ScrollLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    ScrollLayout.Parent = Scroll
    Instance.new("UIPadding", Scroll).PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)

    local function AddTargetUI(p)
        if p == LocalPlayer then return end
        local Btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        Btn.Name = p.Name
        Btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 32)
        Btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
        Btn.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Bg
        Btn.Text = "   > " .. p.DisplayName .. " (@" .. p.Name .. ")"
        Btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        Btn.TextSize = 11
        Btn.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
        Btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        Btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        Btn.Parent = Scroll
        Instance.new("UICorner", Btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        local VisDot = Instance.new("Frame")
        VisDot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
        VisDot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -4)
        VisDot.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SubText
        VisDot.Parent = Btn
        Instance.new("UICorner", VisDot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            State.SpecificPlayers[p.Name] = not State.SpecificPlayers[p.Name]
            local isH = State.SpecificPlayers[p.Name]
            pcall(function() ApplyPlayerState(p) end)
            if State.DataSuppressor then UpdateChatSuppressionForPlayer(p) end
            TweenService:Create(VisDot, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = isH and Theme.Primary or Theme.SubText}):Play()
            LogTerminal(isH and ("TARGET HIDDEN: " .. p.Name) or ("TARGET VISIBLE: " .. p.Name), isH and Theme.Primary or Theme.SubText)
        end)
    end

    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do AddTargetUI(p) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(AddTargetUI)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
        if Scroll:FindFirstChild(p.Name) then Scroll[p.Name]:Destroy() end
        State.SpecificPlayers[p.Name] = nil
        State.ChatSuppressed[p.Name] = nil
        State.HiddenChatElements[p.Name] = nil
    end)
    ScrollLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        Scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, ScrollLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 8)
    end)

    local function MakeDraggableAndClickable(triggerUI, targetUI, clickCallback)
        local dragToggle, dragStart, startPos, hasMoved = false, nil, nil, false
        triggerUI.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragToggle, dragStart, startPos, hasMoved = true, input.Position, targetUI.Position, false
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragToggle = false
                        if not hasMoved and clickCallback then clickCallback() end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        triggerUI.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and dragToggle then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                if delta.Magnitude > 5 then hasMoved = true end
                local newPos = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                TweenService:Create(targetUI, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Position = newPos}):Play()
            end
        end)
    end

    MakeDraggableAndClickable(TopButton, MainFrame, nil)
    MakeDraggableAndClickable(IconButton, IconContainer, function()
        State.IsOpen = not State.IsOpen
        if State.IsOpen then
            MainFrame.Visible = true
            MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 320)
            MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TweenService:Create(MainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 520, 0, 410),
                BackgroundTransparency = 0
            }):Play()
            LogTerminal("PANEL OPENED", Theme.Green)
        else
            local tw = TweenService:Create(MainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 320),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1
            })
            tw:Play()
            tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                if not State.IsOpen then MainFrame.Visible = false end
            end)
        end
    end)

    LogTerminal("TRO + WAVOR OVERLORD V12.4 SECURED", Theme.Secondary)
    return ScreenGui
end

local ScreenGui = CreateGUI()

-- ========================================================
-- 4. أنظمة الإخفاء والقمع
-- ========================================================

local originalTransparency = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local originalEnabled = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local originalVolume = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local monitoredElements = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

function IsPlayerHidden(p)
    if not p then return false end
    if p == LocalPlayer then return State.HideSelf end
    return State.HideAll or (State.SpecificPlayers[p.Name] == true)
end

function ApplyToInstance(v, hidden)
    if not v then return end
    if v:IsA("BasePart") or v:IsA("MeshPart") then
        v.LocalTransparencyModifier = hidden and 1 or 0
    elseif v:IsA("Decal") or v:IsA("Texture") then
        if hidden then
            if not originalTransparency[v] then originalTransparency[v] = v.Transparency end
            v.Transparency = 1
        else
            if originalTransparency[v] then
                v.Transparency = originalTransparency[v]
                originalTransparency[v] = nil
            else
                v.Transparency = 0
            end
        end
    elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("Smoke") or v:IsA("Sparkles") or v:IsA("Light") or v:IsA("SurfaceGui") or v:IsA("BillboardGui") or v:IsA("Highlight") or v:IsA("Beam") or v:IsA("Trail") or v:IsA("PointLight") or v:IsA("SpotLight") or v:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
        if hidden then
            if not originalEnabled[v] then originalEnabled[v] = v.Enabled end
            v.Enabled = false
        else
            if originalEnabled[v] then
                v.Enabled = originalEnabled[v]
                originalEnabled[v] = nil
            else
                v.Enabled = true
            end
        end
    elseif v:IsA("Sound") then
        if hidden then
            if not originalVolume[v] then originalVolume[v] = v.Volume end
            v.Volume = 0
            v:Stop()
        else
            if originalVolume[v] then
                v.Volume = originalVolume[v]
                originalVolume[v] = nil
            else
                v.Volume = 0.5
            end
        end
    end
end

function ApplyToPlayer(p, hidden)
    if not p then return end
    local char = p.Character
    if char then
        for _, v in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do ApplyToInstance(v, hidden) end
    end
    local bp = p:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") or tool:IsA("HopperBin") then
                for _, v in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do ApplyToInstance(v, hidden) end
            end
        end
    end
end

function UpdateHiddenPlayersList()
    State.HiddenPlayers = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if IsPlayerHidden(p) then table.insert(State.HiddenPlayers, p) end
    end
end

function ApplyPlayerState(p)
    ApplyToPlayer(p, IsPlayerHidden(p))
    UpdateHiddenPlayersList()
end

function ContainsTargetIdentifier(text, target)
    if not text or text == "" then return false end
    local lower = text:lower()
    if target.Name and string.find(lower, target.Name:lower(), 1, true) then return true end
    if target.DisplayName and string.find(lower, target.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true) then return true end
    if target.UserId and string.find(lower, tostring(target.UserId), 1, true) then return true end
    return false
end

function IsElementRelatedToPlayer(elem, p)
    if elem:IsA("TextLabel") or elem:IsA("TextButton") or elem:IsA("TextBox") then
        local text = elem.Text or elem.ContentText or ""
        if ContainsTargetIdentifier(text, p) then return true end
    end
    if ContainsTargetIdentifier(elem.Name, p) then return true end
    for _, attr in ipairs(elem:GetAttributes()) do
        if ContainsTargetIdentifier(tostring(elem:GetAttribute(attr)), p) then return true end
    end
    if p.Character and elem:IsDescendantOf(p.Character) then return true end
    local bp = p:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp and elem:IsDescendantOf(bp) then return true end
    return false
end

function SetElementHidden(elem, hidden, targetName)
    if hidden then
        if not State.HiddenChatElements[targetName] then State.HiddenChatElements[targetName] = {} end
        local found = false
        for _, e in ipairs(State.HiddenChatElements[targetName]) do
            if e == elem then found = true break end
        end
        if not found then table.insert(State.HiddenChatElements[targetName], elem) end
        elem.Visible = false
    else
        elem.Visible = true
    end
end

function ScanParentForTargets(parent, targetMap)
    if not parent then return end
    for _, elem in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
        for targetName, p in pairs(targetMap) do
            if IsElementRelatedToPlayer(elem, p) then
                SetElementHidden(elem, true, targetName)
                break
            end
        end
    end
end

function MonitorElementChanges(elem)
    if not (elem:IsA("TextLabel") or elem:IsA("TextButton") or elem:IsA("TextBox") or elem:IsA("Frame") or elem:IsA("ImageLabel") or elem:IsA("ImageButton")) then return end
    if monitoredElements[elem] then return end
    monitoredElements[elem] = true
    local function recheck()
        if not State.DataSuppressor then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            for targetName, p in pairs(State.ChatSuppressed) do
                if p and IsElementRelatedToPlayer(elem, p) then
                    SetElementHidden(elem, true, targetName)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    if elem:IsA("TextLabel") or elem:IsA("TextButton") or elem:IsA("TextBox") then
        elem:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(recheck)
    end
    elem:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Name"):Connect(recheck)
end

function SetChatSuppressed(p, active)
    State.ChatSuppressed[p.Name] = active
    if active then
        local targetMap = {[p.Name] = p}
        ScanParentForTargets(LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"), targetMap)
        pcall(function() ScanParentForTargets(CoreGui, targetMap) end)
        ScanParentForTargets(Workspace, targetMap)
    else
        if State.HiddenChatElements[p.Name] then
            for _, elem in ipairs(State.HiddenChatElements[p.Name]) do
                if elem and elem.Parent then elem.Visible = true end
            end
            State.HiddenChatElements[p.Name] = nil
        end
    end
end

function UpdateChatSuppressionForPlayer(p)
    local shouldSuppress = State.DataSuppressor and IsPlayerHidden(p)
    local current = State.ChatSuppressed[p.Name] == true
    if shouldSuppress ~= current then
        SetChatSuppressed(p, shouldSuppress)
    end
end

function ApplyDataSuppressorToAll()
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        UpdateChatSuppressionForPlayer(p)
    end
end

function OnNewElement(v)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() MonitorElementChanges(v) end)
        if not State.DataSuppressor then return end
        task.wait(0.1)
        for targetName, p in pairs(State.ChatSuppressed) do
            if p and IsElementRelatedToPlayer(v, p) then
                SetElementHidden(v, true, targetName)
                break
            end
        end
    end)
end

pcall(function() LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui").DescendantAdded:Connect(OnNewElement) end)
pcall(function() CoreGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(OnNewElement) end)
pcall(function() Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(OnNewElement) end)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if State.DataSuppressor then
            for targetName, elements in pairs(State.HiddenChatElements) do
                if Players:FindFirstChild(targetName) then
                    for _, elem in ipairs(elements) do
                        if elem and elem.Parent and elem.Visible then
                            elem.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.75)
    end
end)

for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Character"):Connect(function()
        if IsPlayerHidden(p) then
            task.wait(0.2)
            ApplyToPlayer(p, true)
        end
    end)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
    p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Character"):Connect(function()
        if IsPlayerHidden(p) then
            task.wait(0.2)
            ApplyToPlayer(p, true)
        end
    end)
end)

end)

                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

local protectionActive = false

local charAddedConnection = nil
local frameConnection = nil
local blockedCount = 0

-- ========== دالة إيقاف الأنيميشن (سريعة وبدون لاق) ==========
local function ForceStop(track)
    pcall(function()
        track:AdjustWeight(0) 
        track:Stop()
    end)
end

local function applyAntiAnim(character)
    if not character or not protectionActive then return end
    
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return end
    
    local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end

    -- تعطيل سكربت الحركة الأساسي لتثبيت الشخصية
    local animateScript = character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if animateScript then
        animateScript.Disabled = true
    end

    -- إيقاف أي أنيميشن قيد التشغيل حالياً
    for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        ForceStop(track)
        blockedCount = blockedCount + 1
    end

    -- مراقبة الأنيميشن الجديد فور حدوثه
    if animConnection then animConnection:Disconnect() end
    animConnection = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
        if not protectionActive then return end
        ForceStop(track)
        blockedCount = blockedCount + 1
    end)
end

-- ========== دوال التشغيل والإيقاف ==========
local function enableProtection()
    protectionActive = true
    
    if player.Character then
        applyAntiAnim(player.Character)
    end
    
    if charAddedConnection then charAddedConnection:Disconnect() end
    charAddedConnection = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(0.3)
        applyAntiAnim(char)
    end)

    -- جدار حماية خفيف مرتبط بالفريمات لمنع أي ثغرة تجاوز (Zero-Lag RenderStepped)
    if frameConnection then frameConnection:Disconnect() end
    frameConnection = runService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not protectionActive then return end
        local char = player.Character
        if char then
            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid then
                local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if animator then
                    for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                        ForceStop(track)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function disableProtection()
    protectionActive = false
    
    if animConnection then
        animConnection:Disconnect()
        animConnection = nil
    end
    if charAddedConnection then
        charAddedConnection:Disconnect()
        charAddedConnection = nil
    end
    if frameConnection then
        frameConnection:Disconnect()
        frameConnection = nil
    end

    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        local animateScript = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if animateScript then
            animateScript.Disabled = false
        end
    end
end

-- ========== دوال التفاعل والتأثيرات الخفيفة ==========
local function startContinuousProgressBar()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local t1 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            t1:Play()
            t1.Completed:Wait()
            if not protectionActive then break end
            
            local t2 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
            t2:Play()
            t2.Completed:Wait()
        end
        tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    end)
end

local function startTerminalLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local cyberOperations = {
                "> [ANTI-EMOTE] Disabling default AnimateScript...",
                "> [ANIMATION] Zero-Lag Animator hooks active...",
                "> [PURGE] Track weight adjusted to 0...",
                "> [SHIELD] Nullifying external emote execution.",
                "> [INTEGRITY] Character stance set to Statue Mode."
            }

            local randomPhrase = cyberOperations[math.random(1, #cyberOperations)]
            terminalBox.Text = randomPhrase .. "\n" .. terminalBox.Text
            
            local lines = string.split(terminalBox.Text, "\n")
            if #lines > 5 then
                table.remove(lines, #lines)
                terminalBox.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            end
            
            task.wait(1) 
        end
    end)
end

local function startGlitchLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local randomHex = string.format("%08X", math.random(0, 4294967295))
            interceptLabel.Text = "ANIMATIONS BLOCKED: " .."[".. blockedCount .."]" .. " | HASH: 0x" .. randomHex
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
end

-- زر التشغيل الرئيسي
local function toggleProtectionWithEffect()
    if isProcessing then return end
    isProcessing = true

    if not protectionActive then
        protectBtn.Text = "⚙️ INJECTING ANTI-EMOTE MATRIX..."
        
        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 5, 15)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 80, 255)}):Play()
        
        statusLabel.Text = "ACCESS: ROOT SECURED | STATUE MODE ACTIVE"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 150, 255)
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        enableProtection()

        startContinuousProgressBar()
        startTerminalLoop()
        startGlitchLoop()

        task.wait(0.7)
        protectBtn.Text = "⏹️ ABORT PROTECTION PROTOCOL"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 20, 70)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 255)}):Play()

    else
        protectBtn.Text = "DECOMPILING..."
        terminalBox.Text = "> Purging Anti-Emote hooks...\n> Re-enabling standard animation script..."
        
        task.wait(0.8)
        disableProtection()

        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(140, 50, 255)}):Play()
        
        interceptLabel.Text = "ANIMATIONS BLOCKED: [ 0x00 ]"
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 150, 220)
        
        terminalBox.Text = "> Host online. Ready for Anti-Emote execution..."
        statusLabel.Text = "CLEARANCE: GUEST | STANCE: VULNERABLE"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 120, 160)
        
        protectBtn.Text = "🛡️ INITIATE ANTI-EMOTE GRID"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 20, 35)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(160, 60, 255)}):Play()
    end

    isProcessing = false
end

protectBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleProtectionWithEffect)

icon.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = iconContainer.Position
    end
end)
uis.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        iconContainer.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)
uis.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = false
    end
end)

local IconID = "rbxassetid://127399346559581" 
local protectionActive = false
local isProcessing = false

-- محركات الطيران الممنوعة
local badMovers = {
    BodyMover = true, BodyThrust = true, BodyVelocity = true,
    BodyAngularVelocity = true, LinearVelocity = true,
    AngularVelocity = true, RocketPropulsion = true
}

-- ========== النواة الدفاعية الفائقة (أقصى قوة بدون لاق) ==========
local function enforceAbsolutePhysics()
    if not protectionActive or not player.Character then return end
    
    local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end

    -- 1. الكثافة الخرسانية: جعل وزن اللاعب عملاق لصد أي محاولة فلينق
    if root.CustomPhysicalProperties == nil or root.CustomPhysicalProperties.Density ~= 100 then
        root.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(100, 0.3, 0.5, 1, 1)
    end

    -- 2. تنظيف أدوات الطيران الإجباري
    for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
        if badMovers[child.ClassName] then child:Destroy() end
    end

    -- 3. تصفير السرعات المرتفعة
    local linVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    if linVel.Magnitude > 50 or linVel.X ~= linVel.X then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, linVel.Y, 0)
    end

    local angVel = root.AssemblyAngularVelocity
    if angVel.Magnitude > 50 or angVel.X ~= angVel.X then
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
end

runService.Stepped:Connect(enforceAbsolutePhysics)
runService.Heartbeat:Connect(enforceAbsolutePhysics)

-- حلقة إلغاء التصادم المحسنة (بدون لاق)

    while task.wait(0.1) do
        if protectionActive then
            for _, p in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= player and p.Character then
                    for _, part in ipairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            part.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function startContinuousProgressBar()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local t1 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            t1:Play()
            t1.Completed:Wait()
            if not protectionActive then break end
            
            local t2 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
            t2:Play()
            t2.Completed:Wait()
        end
        tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    end)
end

local function startTerminalLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local cyberOperations = {
                "> [PHYSICS] Density set to MAXIMUM (100).",
                "> [DEFENSE] Post-Simulation Shield Active.",
                "> [SHIELD] Ghost Mode (NoCollide) Online.",
                "> [CORE] Scrubbing NaN/INF velocity vectors...",
                "> [ANTI-FLING] Kinetic forces absorbed."
            }

            local randomPhrase = cyberOperations[math.random(1, #cyberOperations)]
            terminalBox.Text = randomPhrase .. "\n" .. terminalBox.Text
            
            local lines = string.split(terminalBox.Text, "\n")
            if #lines > 5 then
                table.remove(lines, #lines)
                terminalBox.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            end
            
            task.wait(1.5)
        end
    end)
end

local function startGlitchLoop()
    local vectorsBlocked = math.random(100, 500)
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            vectorsBlocked = vectorsBlocked + math.random(1, 15)
            local randomHex = string.format("%08X", math.random(0, 4294967295))
            
            interceptLabel.Text = "FLING BLOCKED: " .. vectorsBlocked .. " | HASH: 0x" .. randomHex
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

local function toggleProtectionWithEffect()
    if isProcessing then return end
    isProcessing = true

    if not protectionActive then
        protectionActive = true 

        protectBtn.Text = "⚙️ ACTIVATING KINETIC SHIELD..."
        
        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(2, 8, 2)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainLogoStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(divider, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(devsLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 180, 80)}):Play()
        
        statusLabel.Text = "FLING STATUS: SECURED | MASS: INFINITE"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 100)

        startContinuousProgressBar()
        startTerminalLoop()
        startGlitchLoop()

        task.wait(0.7)
        protectBtn.Text = "⏹️ ABORT ANTI-FLING"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 50, 20)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)}):Play()

    else
        protectionActive = false
        protectBtn.Text = "DEACTIVATING..."
        terminalBox.Text = "> Purging kinetic shields...\n> Re-establishing normal physics flow..."
        
        if player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.7, 0.3, 0.5, 1, 1)
        end

        task.wait(0.8)

        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 12, 5)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 120, 40)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainLogoStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 120, 40)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(divider, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 120, 40)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(devsLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 160, 70)}):Play()
        
        interceptLabel.Text = "PHYSICS ENGINE: [ VULNERABLE ]"
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 100)
        
        terminalBox.Text = "> Anti-Fling module online. Awaiting execution..."
        statusLabel.Text = "FLING STATUS: DANGER | MASS: NORMAL"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
        
        protectBtn.Text = "🛡️ INITIATE ANTI-FLING"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 40, 15)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 150, 50)}):Play()
    end

    isProcessing = false
end

local dragging, dragStart, startPos
icon.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = iconContainer.Position
    end
end)
uis.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        iconContainer.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)
uis.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = false
    end
end)

-- ========== دالة السبام (أقصى قوة بدون لاق) ==========
local function toggleSpamLogic()
    if protectionActive then
        local remote = rs:FindFirstChild("ResetSkin")
        if remote then
            -- استخدام RenderStepped لربط السبام بالفريمات لأقصى سرعة بدون ضغط على الـ Thread
            spamConnection = runService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                remote:FireServer()
            end)
        else
            warn("[TRO PROTECTION] ResetSkin remote not found in ReplicatedStorage!")
        end
    else
        -- إيقاف السبام فوراً عند التعطيل
        if spamConnection then
            spamConnection:Disconnect()
            spamConnection = nil
        end
    end
end

local tickTime = 0
runService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    tickTime = tickTime + (dt * 0.15)
    local hue = tickTime % 1
    local rainbowColor = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.8, 1)
    
    protectBtn.BackgroundColor3 = rainbowColor
    mainStroke.Color = rainbowColor
    mainLogoStroke.Color = rainbowColor
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = rainbowColor
    btnStroke.Color = rainbowColor
    iconStroke.Color = rainbowColor
    termStroke.Color = rainbowColor
    
    title.TextColor3 = rainbowColor
    devsLabel.TextColor3 = rainbowColor
    interceptLabel.TextColor3 = rainbowColor
    terminalBox.TextColor3 = rainbowColor
    label.TextColor3 = rainbowColor 
    
    progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = rainbowColor
    
    if protectionActive then
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = rainbowColor
    end
end)

local function startContinuousProgressBar()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local t1 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            t1:Play()
            t1.Completed:Wait()
            if not protectionActive then break end
            
            local t2 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
            t2:Play()
            t2.Completed:Wait()
        end
        tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    end)
end

local function startSimpleCounter()
    task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        while protectionActive do
            count = count + 1
            interceptLabel.Text = "PACKETS SENT: " .. (count * 60)
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)
end

-- زر التشغيل الرئيسي
local function toggleProtectionWithEffect()
    if isProcessing then return end
    isProcessing = true

    if not protectionActive then
        protectionActive = true 

        protectBtn.Text = "⚡ SPAMMING ResetSkin..."
        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(2, 4, 10)}):Play()
        
        statusLabel.Text = "ACCESS: ROOT SECURED | STATUS: ACTIVE"
        
        terminalBox.Text = "> ANTI-PLAYER System Initiated...\n> Bypassing remote protocols...\n> Spamming [ResetSkin] successful.\n> Holding connection steady."

        toggleSpamLogic() 
        startContinuousProgressBar()
        startSimpleCounter()

        task.wait(0.7)
        protectBtn.Text = "⏹️ ABORT PROTOCOL"
    else
        protectionActive = false
        protectBtn.Text = "DECOMPILING..."
        terminalBox.Text = "> Stopping spam packets...\n> Re-establishing normal thread flow..."
        
        toggleSpamLogic() 

        task.wait(0.8)

        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(4, 8, 18)}):Play()

local presets = {}
local nameMode = "وهمي"
local normalLetterCount = 2
local infiniteSpam = false

local function loadSettings()
    if isfile and isfile(FileName) and readfile then
        local suc, dec = pcall(function() return http:JSONDecode(readfile(FileName)) end)
        if suc and type(dec) == "table" then
            if dec.presets then presets = dec.presets else presets = dec end
            if dec.nameMode then nameMode = dec.nameMode end
            if dec.normalLetterCount then normalLetterCount = dec.normalLetterCount end
            if dec.infiniteSpam ~= nil then infiniteSpam = dec.infiniteSpam end
        end
    end
    if #presets == 0 then
        for _, v in ipairs(defaultPresets) do table.insert(presets, {name = v.name, icon = v.icon, pattern = v.pattern}) end
    end
end

local function saveSettings()
    if writefile then
        pcall(function()
            local data = { presets = presets, nameMode = nameMode, normalLetterCount = normalLetterCount, infiniteSpam = infiniteSpam }
            writefile(FileName, http:JSONEncode(data))
        end)
    end
end
loadSettings()

local function formatTargetName(rawName)
    if nameMode == "وهمي" then return rawName
    elseif nameMode == "طبيعي" then return string.sub(rawName, 1, normalLetterCount)
    end
    return rawName
end

-- ========== الريموتات ==========
local cachedDS, cachedHD, cachedCmdMod = nil, nil, nil

local function getDataService()
    if cachedDS and cachedDS.Parent then return cachedDS end
    pcall(function()
        cachedDS = rs:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvents") and rs.RemoteEvents:FindFirstChild("DataService")
        if not cachedDS then cachedDS = rs:FindFirstChild("DataService", true) or rs:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents") end
    end)
    return cachedDS
end

local function getHDAdminRemote()
    if cachedHD and cachedHD.Parent then return cachedHD end
    pcall(function()
        local hd = rs:FindFirstChild("HDAdminHDClient", true) or rs:FindFirstChild("HDAdminClient", true)
        if hd then
            local sig = hd:FindFirstChild("Signals") or hd:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            if sig then cachedHD = sig:FindFirstChild("RequestCommand") or sig:FindFirstChild("ExecCmd") end
        end
        if not cachedHD then cachedHD = rs:FindFirstChild("RequestCommand", true) end
    end)
    return cachedHD
end

local function getCmdModRemote()
    if cachedCmdMod and cachedCmdMod.Parent then return cachedCmdMod end
    pcall(function()
        local hd = rs:FindFirstChild("HDAdminHDClient", true)
        if hd and hd:FindFirstChild("Signals") then cachedCmdMod = hd.Signals:FindFirstChild("RequestCommandModification") end
    end)
    return cachedCmdMod
end

-- ========== إعدادات الإرسال ==========
local targetStates = { NormalChat = true, HiddenChat = false, HDAdmin = false }
local selectedSpeed = 0.5

local function sendNormal(text)
    if not targetStates.NormalChat then return end
    local ds = getDataService()
    if ds then
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                if ds:IsA("RemoteEvent") then ds:FireServer(text)
                elseif ds:IsA("Folder") and ds:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest") then ds.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(text, "All") end
            end)
        end)
    end
    if text:sub(1,1) == "/" then
        local cmdMod = getCmdModRemote()
        if cmdMod then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    if cmdMod:IsA("RemoteEvent") then cmdMod:FireServer(text)
                    elseif cmdMod:IsA("RemoteFunction") then cmdMod:InvokeServer(text) end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function sendHidden(text)
    if not targetStates.HiddenChat then return end
    local hd = getHDAdminRemote()
    if hd then
        pcall(function() hd:InvokeServer("CmdBar", text) end)
        pcall(function() hd:InvokeServer("SilentCmd", text) end)
        pcall(function() hd:InvokeServer(text) end)
        pcall(function() hd:InvokeServer("Cmd", text) end)
    end
    pcall(function()
        local legacyChat = rs:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
        if legacyChat and legacyChat:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest") then
            legacyChat.SayMessageRequest:FireServer("/e " .. text, "All")
        end
    end)
end

local function sendHDAdminDirect(text)
    if not targetStates.HDAdmin then return end
    local hd = getHDAdminRemote()
    if hd then
        pcall(function() hd:InvokeServer(text) end)
        pcall(function() hd:InvokeServer("Cmd", text) end)
        pcall(function() hd:InvokeServer("CmdBar", text) end)
    end
end

-- ========== حذف الصوتيات ==========
local audioDescendantConn = nil
local function nukeAudioInstances()
    task.spawn(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj.Name == "Audio" then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
        end
        if isfile and listfiles and delfile then
            pcall(function()
                if isfile("Audio") then delfile("Audio") end
                for _, file in ipairs(listfiles()) do
                    if string.match(file, "Audio") then pcall(function() delfile(file) end) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end
local function startAudioAutoNuke()
    nukeAudioInstances()
    if not audioDescendantConn then
        audioDescendantConn = game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if obj.Name == "Audio" then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
        end)
    end
end
local function stopAudioAutoNuke()
    if audioDescendantConn then audioDescendantConn:Disconnect(); audioDescendantConn = nil end
end

local function updatePlayerList()
    for _, b in pairs(scrollList:GetChildren()) do if b:IsA("TextButton") then b:Destroy() end end
    local players = game.Players:GetPlayers()
    scrollList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.ceil(#players/2) * 21)

    for _, plr in ipairs(players) do
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", scrollList)
        local isSelected = selectedPlayers[plr.Name] ~= nil
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = isSelected and Color3.fromRGB(200, 40, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(25, 6, 6)
        btn.Text = " 🎯 " .. plr.Name
        btn.TextColor3 = isSelected and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        btn.TextSize = 8.5
        btn.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not multiTargetMode then selectedPlayers = {} end
            if selectedPlayers[plr.Name] then selectedPlayers[plr.Name] = nil else selectedPlayers[plr.Name] = true end

            local count = 0; for _ in pairs(selectedPlayers) do count = count + 1 end
            if count == 0 then
                selectedLabel.Text = "❌ NO TARGET"
                selectedLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 150, 150)
            elseif count == 1 then
                selectedLabel.Text = "🎯 TARGET: " .. formatTargetName(next(selectedPlayers))
                selectedLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
            else
                selectedLabel.Text = "🎯 TARGETS: (" .. count .. ")"
                selectedLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
            end
            updatePlayerList()
        end)
    end
end

modeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    multiTargetMode = not multiTargetMode
    selectedPlayers = {}
    modeBtn.Text = multiTargetMode and "👥 TARGET RADAR | وضع الاستهداف: متعدد" or "👥 TARGET RADAR | وضع الاستهداف: فردي"
    modeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = multiTargetMode and Color3.fromRGB(80, 20, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(30, 8, 8)
    updatePlayerList()
end)

local function refreshMainPresets()
    for _, v in pairs(presetMainScroll:GetChildren()) do if v:IsA("Frame") then v:Destroy() end end
    local totalWidth = 0
    for i, pData in ipairs(presets) do
        local pFrame = Instance.new("Frame", presetMainScroll)
        pFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 1, -5)
        pFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        totalWidth = totalWidth + 59

        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", pFrame)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = defaultColors[(i-1) % #defaultColors + 1]
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        btn.Text = pData.icon or "⚡"
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 11
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        Instance.new("UIStroke", btn).Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 40, 40)

        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", pFrame)
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 26)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.Text = pData.name
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        nameLabel.TextSize = 8
        nameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local list = {}
            for pName in pairs(selectedPlayers) do table.insert(list, formatTargetName(pName)) end
            local targets = table.concat(list, ",")
            if targets == "" then return end
            textBox.Text = string.gsub(pData.pattern, "me", targets):gsub("%s+$", "")
        end)
    end
    presetMainScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, totalWidth, 0, 0)
end

local function refreshEditPresets()
    for _, v in pairs(editPresetScroll:GetChildren()) do if v:IsA("TextButton") then v:Destroy() end end
    local totalWidth = 0
    delPrepBtn.Visible = (selectedPrepEdit > 5)
    for i, pData in ipairs(presets) do
        local eBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", editPresetScroll)
        eBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 24)
        eBtn.Text = pData.icon .. " " .. pData.name
        eBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        eBtn.TextSize = 9
        eBtn.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        Instance.new("UICorner", eBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        Instance.new("UIStroke", eBtn).Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 20, 20)
        totalWidth = totalWidth + 61
        if i == selectedPrepEdit then
            eBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 20, 20)
            eBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
        else
            eBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 8, 8)
            eBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        end
        eBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            selectedPrepEdit = i
            refreshEditPresets()
            nameEditor.Text = presets[i].name
            iconEditor.Text = presets[i].icon
            editorInput.Text = presets[i].pattern
        end)
    end
    local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", editPresetScroll)
    addBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 24)
    addBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 100, 40)
    addBtn.Text = "+"
    addBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    addBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    addBtn.TextSize = 14
    Instance.new("UICorner", addBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    Instance.new("UIStroke", addBtn).Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 180, 60)
    totalWidth = totalWidth + 31
    addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local nextNum = #presets + 1
        table.insert(presets, { name = "تجهيز " .. nextNum, icon = "⚡", pattern = "/re me" })
        selectedPrepEdit = #presets
        saveSettings()
        refreshEditPresets()
        refreshMainPresets()
        nameEditor.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].name
        iconEditor.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].icon
        editorInput.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].pattern
        task.wait(0.05)
        editPresetScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(editPresetScroll.CanvasSize.X.Offset, 0)
    end)
    editPresetScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, totalWidth, 0, 0)
end

savePrepBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if presets[selectedPrepEdit] then
        presets[selectedPrepEdit].name = nameEditor.Text
        presets[selectedPrepEdit].icon = iconEditor.Text ~= "" and iconEditor.Text or "⚡"
        presets[selectedPrepEdit].pattern = editorInput.Text
        saveSettings()
        refreshEditPresets()
        refreshMainPresets()
        savePrepBtn.Text = "تم الحفظ بنجاح! ✔️"
        savePrepBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 100, 40)
        task.wait(1.5)
        savePrepBtn.Text = "حفظ التغييرات 💾"
        savePrepBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 60, 100)
    end
end)

delPrepBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if selectedPrepEdit <= 5 then
        showNotification("حماية النسخ", "النسخ 1 إلى 5 محمية من الحذف، يمكنك التعديل فقط!", 3)
        return
    end
    if #presets > 5 then
        table.remove(presets, selectedPrepEdit)
        selectedPrepEdit = math.max(1, selectedPrepEdit - 1)
        saveSettings()
        refreshEditPresets()
        refreshMainPresets()
        nameEditor.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].name
        iconEditor.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].icon
        editorInput.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].pattern
    else
        delPrepBtn.Text = "❌"
        task.wait(1)
        delPrepBtn.Text = "🗑️"
    end
end)

refreshMainPresets()
refreshEditPresets()
if presets[selectedPrepEdit] then
    nameEditor.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].name
    iconEditor.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].icon
    editorInput.Text = presets[selectedPrepEdit].pattern
end

local function createToggleSwitch(yPos, labelTxt, key)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", settingsTabContainer)
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 24)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, yPos, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 5, 5)
    btn.Text = ""
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    Instance.new("UIStroke", btn).Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 20, 20)

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.02, 0, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = labelTxt
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    lbl.TextSize = 9
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    local indicator = Instance.new("Frame", btn)
    indicator.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    indicator.Position = UDim2.new(0.9, -10, 0.5, -8)
    indicator.BackgroundColor3 = targetStates[key] and Color3.fromRGB(40, 180, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(80, 20, 20)
    Instance.new("UICorner", indicator).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        targetStates[key] = not targetStates[key]
        indicator.BackgroundColor3 = targetStates[key] and Color3.fromRGB(40, 180, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(80, 20, 20)
    end)
end

    lBtn.Text = tostring(i)
    lBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

    lBtn.TextColor3 = (normalLetterCount == i) and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    Instance.new("UICorner", lBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    Instance.new("UIStroke", lBtn).Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 20, 20)
    letterButtons[i] = lBtn
    lBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        normalLetterCount = i
        for num, btn in ipairs(letterButtons) do
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = (num == i) and Color3.fromRGB(180, 20, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(25, 6, 6)
            btn.TextColor3 = (num == i) and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        end

    end)
end

            b.BackgroundColor3 = (j == i) and Color3.fromRGB(180, 20, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(20, 5, 5)
            b.TextColor3 = (j == i) and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end
    end)
end

-- ========== محرك السبام ==========
local spamming = false
local spamThreadNormal = nil
local pulseTween1, pulseTween2
local keepSpamming = false

local function applyCyberAttackVisuals(active)
    if active then
        isShaking = true
        local tInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true)
        pulseTween1 = tweens:Create(frame, tInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 0, 0)})
        pulseTween2 = tweens:Create(mainStroke, tInfo, {Color = Color3.fromRGB(220, 0, 0), Thickness = 4})
        pulseTween1:Play(); pulseTween2:Play()
        matrixBackground.TextTransparency = 0.6
        binaryStream.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)

        local lastUpdate = 0
        renderConnection = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastUpdate >= 0.05 then
                lastUpdate = now
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(basePosition.X.Scale, basePosition.X.Offset + math.random(-1, 1), basePosition.Y.Scale, basePosition.Y.Offset + math.random(-1, 1))
                statusLabel.Text = "STATUS // ATTACKING 🔄"
                statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
            end
        end)
    else
        isShaking = false
        if renderConnection then renderConnection:Disconnect(); renderConnection = nil end
        if pulseTween1 then pulseTween1:Cancel(); pulseTween2:Cancel() end
        frame.Position = basePosition
        matrixBackground.Text = ""
        matrixBackground.TextTransparency = 1
        binaryStream.Text = "AWAITING_UPLINK..."
        binaryStream.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 200)
        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 20, 20), Thickness = 2}):Play()
    end
end

local function stopSpam()
    spamming = false
    keepSpamming = false
    if spamThreadNormal then task.cancel(spamThreadNormal); spamThreadNormal = nil end
    stopAudioAutoNuke()
    applyCyberAttackVisuals(false)
    toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 110, 40)
    toggleBtn.Text = "▶ EXECUTE PROTOCOL"
    toggleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(30, 180, 70)
    statusLabel.Text = "STATUS // STANDBY ⏸"
    statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    showNotification("تنبيه", "تم إيقاف الهجوم.", 3)
end

local function startSpam()
    if spamming or textBox.Text == "" then return end
    startAudioAutoNuke()
    spamming = true
    toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 0, 0)
    toggleBtn.Text = "☠️ FATAL OVERDRIVE ACTIVE ☠️"
    toggleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30)
    applyCyberAttackVisuals(true)
    showNotification("تنبيه", "تم تفعيل الهجوم...", 3.5)

    local msg = textBox.Text
    keepSpamming = true

    spamThreadNormal = task.spawn(function()
        while keepSpamming do
            pcall(function() sendNormal(msg) end)
            pcall(function() sendHidden(msg) end)
            pcall(function() sendHDAdminDirect(msg) end)

            if selectedSpeed == 0 then
                task.wait()
            else
                task.wait(selectedSpeed)
            end
        end
        if spamming then
            spamming = false
            task.wait(0.1)
            stopSpam()
        end
    end)
end

toggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if spamming then
        stopSpam()
    else
        startSpam()
    end
end)

updatePlayerList()
game.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(updatePlayerList)
game.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    if selectedPlayers[plr.Name] then selectedPlayers[plr.Name] = nil end
    updatePlayerList()
end)

-- المطورين: EEU6T و TROLORD6

local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
