local tweens = game:GetService("TweenService")
local uis = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

-- ========== إعدادات الأيقونة والشعار ==========
local IconID = "rbxassetid://120723270430809" 

-- ========== متغيرات الحماية ==========
local protectionActive = false
local isProcessing = false
local activeConnections = {}
local blockedCount = 0
local savedCFrame = nil 

-- ========== نظام مضاعفة الفريمات الأسطوري (Zero-Lag Optimized) ==========
local function maxFPSBoost()
    if not protectionActive then return end
    
    pcall(function() setfpscap(999) end)

    pcall(function()
        lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
        lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0 
        
        for _, v in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("BloomEffect") then
                v.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if workspace.Terrain then
            workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
            workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
            workspace.Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
            workspace.Terrain.Decoration = false
        end
    end)

    local function optimizePart(obj)
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function() obj.CastShadow = false end)
            if obj.Anchored and not obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TouchTransmitter") then
                pcall(function() obj.CanTouch = false end)
            end
        elseif obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            pcall(function() obj.Rate = math.clamp(obj.Rate, 0, 1) end)
        elseif obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") or obj:IsA("Trail") then
            pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if not protectionActive then break end
            optimizePart(obj)
        end
    end)

    local mapOptimizerConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not protectionActive then return end
        task.defer(function()
            optimizePart(obj)
        end)
    end)
    table.insert(activeConnections, mapOptimizerConn)

    local memoryCleaner = task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            task.wait(15)
            pcall(function() collectgarbage("collect") end)
        end
    end)
    table.insert(activeConnections, memoryCleaner)
end

-- ========== دوال الحماية فائقة الخفة ==========
local function isSuspicious(el)
    local name = string.lower(el.Name)
    local keywords = {"blood", "damage", "hurt", "death", "red", "vignette", "kill", "flash", "pain", "screen", "overlay"}
    
    for _, k in ipairs(keywords) do
        if string.find(name, k) then return true end
    end
    
    if el:IsA("ImageLabel") or el:IsA("Frame") then
        pcall(function()
            if el.Size.X.Scale >= 1 and el.Size.Y.Scale >= 1 then
                local color = el:IsA("ImageLabel") and el.ImageColor3 or el.BackgroundColor3
                if color.R > 0.6 and color.G < 0.3 and color.B < 0.3 then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    return false
end

local function lockdownElement(el)
    if not protectionActive then return end
    if el.Name == "TROProtection_CyberV3" or el.Name == "TROIcon_CyberV3" then return end
    
    if (el:IsA("GuiObject") or el:IsA("ScreenGui")) and isSuspicious(el) then
        pcall(function()
            if el:IsA("ScreenGui") then el.Enabled = false end
            if el:IsA("GuiObject") then 
                el.Visible = false 
                if el:IsA("ImageLabel") then el.ImageTransparency = 1 end
                if el:IsA("Frame") then el.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
            end
            blockedCount = blockedCount + 1
        end)
    end
end

local function cleanRedLighting()
    for _, effect in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if effect:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then
            if effect.TintColor.R > 0.7 and effect.TintColor.G < 0.4 and effect.TintColor.B < 0.4 then
                effect.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end
end

local function setupCharacter(char)
    if not protectionActive then return end
    
    local humanoidRootPart = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)
    local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
    
    if humanoidRootPart and humanoid then
        humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
        
        local loopThread = task.spawn(function()
            while protectionActive and humanoid.Health > 0 and char.Parent do
                if humanoidRootPart.Position.Y > (workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 5) then
                    savedCFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
                end
                task.wait(0.2) 
            end
        end)
        table.insert(activeConnections, loopThread)

        local dieConn = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            if not protectionActive then return end
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part.Anchored = true       
                    part.CanCollide = false    
                    part.CanTouch = false 
                end
            end
        end)
        table.insert(activeConnections, dieConn)
    end
end

local function enableAntiKill()
    protectionActive = true
    blockedCount = 0
    savedCFrame = nil
    
    task.spawn(maxFPSBoost)
    
    if player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
        for _, el in ipairs(player.PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do
            lockdownElement(el)
        end
        local guiConn = player.PlayerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(lockdownElement)
        table.insert(activeConnections, guiConn)
    end
    
    cleanRedLighting()
    local lightConn = lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.2)
        if protectionActive then cleanRedLighting() end
    end)
    table.insert(activeConnections, lightConn)
    
    if player.Character then
        setupCharacter(player.Character)
    end
    
    local charConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
        if not protectionActive then return end
        
        task.spawn(function()
            local newHRP = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)
            if newHRP and savedCFrame then
                newHRP.CFrame = savedCFrame
                task.wait(0.03) 
                newHRP.CFrame = savedCFrame
            end
        end)
        
        setupCharacter(newChar)
    end)
    table.insert(activeConnections, charConn)
    
    pcall(function() game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Health, false) end)
end

local function disableAntiKill()
    protectionActive = false
    
    for _, conn in ipairs(activeConnections) do
        if type(conn) == "userdata" or typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
        elseif type(conn) == "thread" then
            pcall(function() task.cancel(conn) end)
        end
    end
    activeConnections = {}
    savedCFrame = nil
    
    if player.Character then
        local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = true end
    end
    
    pcall(function() game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Health, true) end)
end

-- ========== دوال التفاعل والتأثيرات ==========
local function startContinuousProgressBar()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local t1 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            t1:Play()
            t1.Completed:Wait()
            if not protectionActive then break end
            
            local t2 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
            t2:Play()
            t2.Completed:Wait()
        end
        tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    end)
end

local function startTerminalLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local cyberOperations = {
                "> [RES] Position saved with zero lag.",
                "> [PERF] Physics calculations optimized.",
                "> [CAM] Smooth camera tracking active.",
                "> [FPS] Zero-lag display booster running.",
                "> [GPU] Heavy lighting bypassed.",
                "> [RAM] Garbage collection active."
            }

            local randomPhrase = cyberOperations[math.random(1, #cyberOperations)]
            terminalBox.Text = randomPhrase .. "\n" .. terminalBox.Text
            
            local lines = string.split(terminalBox.Text, "\n")
            if #lines > 5 then
                table.remove(lines, #lines)
                terminalBox.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            end
            
            task.wait(1.2) 
        end
    end)
end

local function startGlitchLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local randomHex = string.format("%08X", math.random(0, 4294967295))
            interceptLabel.Text = "EFFECTS BLOCKED: [ " .. tostring(blockedCount) .. " ] | HASH: 0x" .. randomHex
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

local function toggleProtectionWithEffect()
    if isProcessing then return end
    isProcessing = true

    if not protectionActive then
        protectBtn.Text = "⚙️ DEPLOYING PROTOCOL..."
        
        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 15, 2)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0)}):Play()
        
        statusLabel.Text = "STATUS: LEGENDARY FPS | RES ACTIVE"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 100)
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)

        enableAntiKill()

        startContinuousProgressBar()
        startTerminalLoop()
        startGlitchLoop()

        task.wait(0.7)
        protectBtn.Text = "⏹️ ABORT SHIELD & RES"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 20, 20)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)}):Play()

    else
        protectBtn.Text = "REVERTING SYSTEM..."
        terminalBox.Text = "> Clearing connections...\n> System returning to default."
        
        task.wait(0.8)
        disableAntiKill()

        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 12, 5)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 100, 20)}):Play()
        
        interceptLabel.Text = "EFFECTS BLOCKED: [ 0 ]"
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 160, 80)
        
        terminalBox.Text = "> System idle. Ready for MAX FPS protocol..."
        statusLabel.Text = "FPS OPTIMIZED | RES: READY"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 80, 80)
        
        protectBtn.Text = "🛡️ INITIATE SHIELD & RES"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 45, 10)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(140, 110, 20)}):Play()
    end

    isProcessing = false
end

protectBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleProtectionWithEffect)

icon.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = iconContainer.Position
    end
end)
uis.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        iconContainer.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)
uis.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = false
    end
end)

        name = 'منع الانتقال',
        icon = '📍',
        title = 'TRO + WAVOR — منع الانتقال',
        source = [=[-- سكربت حماية ANTI-TP / ANTI-APPARATE (الأيقونة الجديدة + أقصى أداء بدون لاق)
-- المطورين: EEU6T و TROLORD6
local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local tweens = game:GetService("TweenService")
local uis = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

-- ========== إعدادات الأيقونة الجديدة ==========
local IconID = "rbxassetid://102325222229723" 

-- ========== متغيرات الحماية ==========
local protectionActive = false
local isProcessing = false
local runServiceConnection = nil
local characterAddedConnection = nil
local lastValidCFrame = nil
local blockedCount = 0

-- ========== دوال منع الانتقال (سريعة وخفيفة على المعالج) ==========
local function enableProtection()
    lastValidCFrame = nil
    
    characterAddedConnection = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
        lastValidCFrame = nil
        task.spawn(function()
            local rootPart = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if rootPart then
                rootPart.Anchored = true
                task.wait(0.4)
                rootPart.Anchored = false
                lastValidCFrame = rootPart.CFrame
            end
        end)
    end)
    
    -- مراقبة خفيفة ومستقرة مرتبطة بالفريمات لمنع أي استهلاك زائد
    runServiceConnection = runService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not protectionActive then return end
        
        local character = player.Character
        if not character then return end 
        
        local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        
        if not rootPart or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then 
            return 
        end

        if not lastValidCFrame then
            lastValidCFrame = rootPart.CFrame
            return
        end

        local distance = (rootPart.Position - lastValidCFrame.Position).Magnitude
        
        if distance > 15 then
            blockedCount = blockedCount + 1
            rootPart.CFrame = lastValidCFrame
            pcall(function()
                rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end)
        else
            lastValidCFrame = rootPart.CFrame
        end
    end)
end

local function disableProtection()
    if runServiceConnection then
        runServiceConnection:Disconnect()
        runServiceConnection = nil
    end
    if characterAddedConnection then
        characterAddedConnection:Disconnect()
        characterAddedConnection = nil
    end
    lastValidCFrame = nil
end

-- ========== دوال التحريك والحلقات ==========
local function startContinuousProgressBar()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local t1 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            t1:Play()
            t1.Completed:Wait()
            if not protectionActive then break end
            
            local t2 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
            t2:Play()
            t2.Completed:Wait()
        end
        tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    end)
end

local function startTerminalLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local cyberOperations = {
                "> [ANTI-TP] Zero-Lag CFrame tracking active.",
                "> [DEFENSE] Blocking unauthorized position shifts...",
                "> [SHIELD] Distance magnitude validation running.",
                "> [INTEGRITY] Player position locked securely."
            }

            local randomPhrase = cyberOperations[math.random(1, #cyberOperations)]
            terminalBox.Text = randomPhrase .. "\n" .. terminalBox.Text
            
            local lines = string.split(terminalBox.Text, "\n")
            if #lines > 7 then
                table.remove(lines, #lines)
                terminalBox.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            end
            
            task.wait(1) 
        end
    end)
end

local function startGlitchLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local randomHex = string.format("%08X", math.random(0, 4294967295))
            interceptLabel.Text = "TP VECTORS BLOCKED: [" .. blockedCount .. "] | HASH: 0x" .. randomHex
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
end

local function toggleProtectionWithEffect()
    if isProcessing then return end
    isProcessing = true

    if not protectionActive then
        protectionActive = true 

        protectBtn.Text = "⚙️ INJECTING ANTI-TP MATRIX..."
        
        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(devsLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)}):Play()
        
        statusLabel.Text = "ACCESS: ROOT SECURED | CFRAME LOCKED"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        enableProtection()

        startContinuousProgressBar()
        startTerminalLoop()
        startGlitchLoop()

        task.wait(0.7)
        protectBtn.Text = "⏹️ ABORT SECURITY PROTOCOL"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()

    else
        protectionActive = false
        protectBtn.Text = "DECOMPILING..."
        terminalBox.Text = "> Purging active Anti-TP hooks...\n> Re-establishing normal physics..."
        
        task.wait(0.8)
        disableProtection()

        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(devsLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)}):Play()
        
        blockedCount = 0
        interceptLabel.Text = "TP VECTORS BLOCKED: [ 0x00 ]"
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        
        terminalBox.Text = "> Host online. Ready for zero-lag Anti-TP..."
        statusLabel.Text = "CLEARANCE: GUEST | CFRAME: VULNERABLE"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)
        
        protectBtn.Text = "🛡️ INITIATE ANTI-TP GRID"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)}):Play()
    end

    isProcessing = false

    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = iconContainer.Position
    end
end)
uis.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        iconContainer.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)
uis.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = false
    end
end)

-- المطورين: EEU6T و TROLORD6

local blockedCount = 0

-- ========== دالة إخفاء الانفجارات اللحظية (سريعة وبدون استهلاك للمعالج) ==========
local function lockVisibility(obj)
    if not protectionActive then return end
    
    if obj.Name == "Explosion" or obj:IsA("Explosion") then
        blockedCount = blockedCount + 1
        if obj:IsA("Explosion") or obj:IsA("GuiObject") then
            obj.Visible = false
            pcall(function()
                obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                    if protectionActive and obj.Visible == true then 
                        obj.Visible = false 
                    end
                end)
            end)
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.Transparency = 1
            pcall(function()
                obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
                    if protectionActive and obj.Transparency < 1 then 
                        obj.Transparency = 1 
                    end
                end)
            end)
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            obj.Enabled = false
            pcall(function()
                obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
                    if protectionActive and obj.Enabled == true then 
                        obj.Enabled = false 
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function enableProtection()
    -- فحص سريع وخفيف للعناصر الموجودة مسبقاً
    task.spawn(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if not protectionActive then break end
            lockVisibility(obj)
        end
    end)
    
    -- التسمع الفوري بأقل استهلاك للموارد
    if not descendantConnection then
        descendantConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if protectionActive then
                lockVisibility(obj)
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function disableProtection()
    if descendantConnection then
        descendantConnection:Disconnect()
        descendantConnection = nil
    end
end

            t2.Completed:Wait()

local theme = themes.blue
local currentThemeKey = "blue"
local isRainbow = false
local rainbowOffset = 0

local function hslToRgb(h, s, l)
    local function hue2rgb(p, q, t)
        if t < 0 then t = t + 1 end
        if t > 1 then t = t - 1 end
        if t < 1/6 then return p + (q - p) * 6 * t end
        if t < 1/2 then return q end
        if t < 2/3 then return p + (q - p) * (2/3 - t) * 6 end
        return p
    end
    if s == 0 then return Color3.new(l, l, l) end
    local q = l < 0.5 and l * (1 + s) or l + s - l * s
    local p = 2 * l - q
    return Color3.new(hue2rgb(p, q, h + 1/3), hue2rgb(p, q, h), hue2rgb(p, q, h - 1/3))
end

local function ensureFolder() if not isfolder(FOLDER) then makefolder(FOLDER) end end
local function serializeCommand(c) return c.text .. "|" .. tostring(c.repeatCount) .. "|" .. (c.template or "") end
local function parseCommandLine(line)
    if line == "" then return nil end
    local cmd, rep, tmpl = line:match("^([^|]+)|([^|]*)|(.*)$")
    if not cmd then return { text = line, repeatCount = DEFAULT_REPEAT, template = "" } end
    local n = tonumber(rep) or DEFAULT_REPEAT
    if n < 1 then n = 1 end
    return { text = cmd, repeatCount = math.floor(n), template = tmpl or "" }
end

local function loadCommands()
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        ensureFolder()
        if not isfile(FILE_COMMANDS) then return nil end
        return readfile(FILE_COMMANDS)
    end)
    if not ok or not result or result == "" then return nil end
    local list = {}
    for line in result:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
        local c = parseCommandLine(line)
        if c then table.insert(list, c) end
    end
    return #list > 0 and list or nil
end

local function saveCommands(list)
    pcall(function()
        ensureFolder()
        local lines = {}
        for _, c in ipairs(list) do table.insert(lines, serializeCommand(c)) end
        writefile(FILE_COMMANDS, table.concat(lines, "\n"))
    end)
end

local function loadNumber(file, default, min)
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        ensureFolder()
        if not isfile(file) then return nil end
        return readfile(file)
    end)
    if not ok or not result then return default end
    local n = tonumber(result)
    if n and n >= (min or 0) then return n end
    return default
end

local function saveNumber(file, n)
    pcall(function() ensureFolder(); writefile(file, tostring(n)) end)
end

local function loadWaterEnabled()
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        ensureFolder()
        if not isfile(FILE_WATER) then return "1" end
        return readfile(FILE_WATER)
    end)
    if not ok or not result then return true end
    return result == "1"
end

local function saveWaterEnabled(enabled)
    pcall(function() ensureFolder(); writefile(FILE_WATER, enabled and "1" or "0") end)
end

local customCommands = loadCommands()
if not customCommands then
    customCommands = {}
    for _, c in ipairs(defaultCommands) do
        table.insert(customCommands, { text = c.text, repeatCount = c.repeatCount, template = c.template })
    end
end
local newCmdDefaultRepeat = loadNumber(FILE_REPEAT, DEFAULT_REPEAT, 1)
local waterEnabled       = loadWaterEnabled()

local function sendCommand(cmd)
    if cmd == "" then return false end
    pcall(function()
        local re = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvents")
        if re then
            local ds = re:FindFirstChild("DataService")
            if ds then ds:FireServer(cmd) end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local hd = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("HDAdminHDClient")
        if hd then
            local sig = hd:FindFirstChild("Signals")
            if sig then
                local req = sig:FindFirstChild("RequestCommandModification")
                if req then req:InvokeServer(cmd) end
            end
        end
    end)
    return true
end

local function expandCustomCommands(input)
    if input == "" or #customCommands == 0 then return input end
    local result = input
    local sorted = {}
    for _, c in ipairs(customCommands) do table.insert(sorted, c) end
    table.sort(sorted, function(a, b) return #a.text > #b.text end)

    for _, cmd in ipairs(sorted) do
        local searchStart = 1
        while true do
            local pos = result:find(cmd.text, searchStart, true)
            if not pos then break end
            local endPos = pos + #cmd.text
            local charAfter = result:sub(endPos, endPos)
            if charAfter == " " or charAfter == "\t" then
                local restAfterSpace = result:sub(endPos + 1)
                local target = restAfterSpace:match("^(%S+)")
                if target and target ~= "" then
                    local actual
                    if cmd.template and cmd.template ~= "" then
                        actual = cmd.template:gsub("{target}", target)
                    else
                        actual = cmd.text .. " " .. target
                    end
                    local before = result:sub(1, pos - 1)
                    local after  = result:sub(endPos + 1 + #target)
                    result = before .. actual .. after
                    searchStart = pos + #actual
                else
                    searchStart = endPos + 1
                end
            else
                searchStart = endPos
            end
        end
    end
    return result
end

local function addGlowStroke(parent, color, thickness, transparency)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", parent)
    s.Color = color; s.Thickness = thickness; s.Transparency = transparency
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    return s
end

local function addOuterGlow(parent, color, thickness, transparency)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", parent)
    s.Color = color; s.Thickness = thickness; s.Transparency = transparency
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    s.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round
    return s
end

local function addBgGradient(parent, c1, c2, rot)
    local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", parent)
    grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c1),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c2),
    })
    grad.Rotation = rot or 90
    return grad
end

local themeRefs = {}
local function reg(obj, role, prop)
    table.insert(themeRefs, { obj = obj, role = role, prop = prop or "BackgroundColor3" })
end

local function applyThemeToAll()
    for _, r in ipairs(themeRefs) do
        if r.obj and r.obj.Parent then
            if r.prop then pcall(function() r.obj[r.prop] = theme[r.role] end) end
            if r.stroke then pcall(function() r.stroke.Color = theme[r.role] end) end
        end
    end
end

screenGui.Name = "MapCopy_Red"
screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = false
screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
screenGui.DisplayOrder = 2147483647
screenGui.Parent = CoreGui
pcall(function() screenGui.Parent = guiParent end)

-- ============ WATER ============
local waterRunning = waterEnabled
local activeDrops = {}

local function spawnWaterDrop(frame)
    if not frame or not frame.Parent then return end
    local size = frame.AbsoluteSize
    local pos = frame.AbsolutePosition
    if size.X <= 0 or size.Y <= 0 then return end

    local side = math.random(1, 4)
    local startX, startY
    if side == 1 then startX = pos.X + math.random() * size.X; startY = pos.Y - 10
    elseif side == 2 then startX = pos.X + size.X + 10; startY = pos.Y + math.random() * size.Y
    elseif side == 3 then startX = pos.X + math.random() * size.X; startY = pos.Y + size.Y + 10
    else startX = pos.X - 10; startY = pos.Y + math.random() * size.Y end

    local dropSize = math.random(14, 26)
    local drop = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    drop.Image = WATER_DECAL
    drop.ImageColor3 = theme.accent
    drop.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    drop.BorderSizePixel = 0
    drop.Size = UDim2.new(0, dropSize, 0, dropSize)
    drop.Position = UDim2.new(0, startX, 0, startY)
    drop.Active = false
    drop.Interactable = false
    drop.ZIndex = 1
    drop.Parent = screenGui

    local dirX = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2
    local dirY = -math.random() * 0.8 - 0.2
    if side == 1 then dirY = -0.7 - math.random() * 0.4
    elseif side == 3 then dirY = 0.7 + math.random() * 0.4 end

    local travel = math.random(70, 180)
    local endX = startX + dirX * travel
    local endY = startY + dirY * travel + math.random(30, 90)
    local duration = math.random(8, 15) / 10

    TweenService:Create(drop,
        TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { Position = UDim2.new(0, endX, 0, endY), ImageTransparency = 1,
          Size = UDim2.new(0, dropSize * 0.3, 0, dropSize * 0.3) }):Play()

    table.insert(activeDrops, drop)
    task.delay(duration + 0.1, function()
        if drop and drop.Parent then drop:Destroy() end
        for i = #activeDrops, 1, -1 do
            if activeDrops[i] == drop then table.remove(activeDrops, i); break end
        end
    end)
end

    task.delay(1.5, function()
        if not isSpamming then setStatus("جاهز", Color3.fromRGB(80,255,180)) end
    end)
end

    end
end)

    return y
end

    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    lbl.TextSize = 10
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.ZIndex = 13
    return y
end

local speedAllButtons = {}

    { label = "x0.5", value = 0.5 },
    { label = "x1", value = 1 },
    { label = "x2", value = 2 },
    { label = "x5", value = 5 },
    { label = "x10", value = 10 },
    { label = "x50", value = 50 },
    { label = "x100", value = 100 },
    { label = "MAX", value = 0 },
}

local function updateSpeedButtons()
    for _, entry in ipairs(speedAllButtons) do
        local isActive = (speedMultiplier == entry.val)
        if isActive then
            entry.btn.BackgroundColor3 = theme.primary
            entry.btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            entry.btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 1, 0)
            entry.btn.TextSize = 12
        else
            entry.btn.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
            entry.btn.TextColor3 = theme.primaryBright
            entry.btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 1, 0)
            entry.btn.TextSize = 10
        end
    end
end

for i, sp in ipairs(speedPresets) do
    local p = Instance.new("TextButton", speedRow)
    p.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 1, 0)
    p.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
    p.Text = sp.label
    p.TextColor3 = theme.primaryBright
    p.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    p.TextSize = 10
    p.AutoButtonColor = false
    p.Active = true

    p.ZIndex = 14
    Instance.new("UICorner", p).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    reg(p, "card")
    reg(p, "primaryBright", "TextColor3")

    table.insert(speedAllButtons, { btn = p, val = sp.value })

    p.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        speedMultiplier = sp.value
        updateSpeedButtons()
        local txt = sp.value <= 0 and "⚡MAX" or ("×" .. tostring(sp.value))
        setStatusLater("سرعة: " .. txt, Color3.fromRGB(80,255,180))
    end)
end

waterToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    waterRunning = not waterRunning
    saveWaterEnabled(waterRunning)
    if waterRunning then
        waterToggle.Text = "مفعل"
        waterToggle.BackgroundColor3 = theme.primary
        task.spawn(function()
            while waterRunning and screenGui.Parent do
                if main and main.Visible then
                    for _ = 1, math.random(1, 3) do spawnWaterDrop(main) end
                end
                if settingsPanel and settingsPanel.Visible then
                    for _ = 1, math.random(1, 3) do spawnWaterDrop(settingsPanel) end
                end
                task.wait(math.random(5, 12) / 100)
            end
        end)
    else
        waterToggle.Text = "معطل"
        waterToggle.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
        for _, d in ipairs(activeDrops) do pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
        activeDrops = {}
    end
end)

-- Logic
local function updatePreview()
    local c = customCommands[editingIndex]
    if not c then return end
    local sample = "Player1"
    local actual
    if eTplInput.Text ~= "" then
        actual = eTplInput.Text:gsub("{target}", sample)
    else
        actual = c.text .. " " .. sample
    end
    local cmdCount = 1
    if eTplInput.Text ~= "" then
        cmdCount = 0
        for _ in eTplInput.Text:gmatch("/%w+") do cmdCount = cmdCount + 1 end
        cmdCount = math.max(cmdCount, 1)
    end
    local total = cmdCount * eRepeatValue
    ePreview.Text = "👁 معاينة:\n" .. actual .. "\n\n📊 " .. cmdCount .. " × " .. eRepeatValue .. " = " .. total .. " أمر"
end

local function setERepeat(n)
    n = tonumber(n) or eRepeatValue
    n = math.floor(n)
    if n < 1 then n = 1 end
    eRepeatValue = n
    eRepInput.Text = tostring(n)
    updatePreview()
end

eMinus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setERepeat(eRepeatValue - 1) end)
ePlus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setERepeat(eRepeatValue + 1) end)
eRepInput.FocusLost:Connect(function()
    local n = tonumber(eRepInput.Text)
    if n then setERepeat(n) else eRepInput.Text = tostring(eRepeatValue) end
end)
eTplInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() updatePreview() end)

local function openEditor(index)
    editingIndex = index
    local c = customCommands[index]
    if not c then return end
    editCmdName.Text = "الامر: " .. c.text
    eRepeatValue = c.repeatCount or newCmdDefaultRepeat
    eRepInput.Text = tostring(eRepeatValue)
    eTplInput.Text = c.template or ""
    updatePreview()
    editOverlay.Visible = true
end

local function closeEditor()
    editingIndex = nil
    editOverlay.Visible = false
end

eSaveBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if not editingIndex then return end
    local c = customCommands[editingIndex]
    if not c then return end
    c.repeatCount = eRepeatValue
    c.template = eTplInput.Text
    saveCommands(customCommands)
    refreshCommandsList()
    closeEditor()
    setStatusLater("✅ حفظ", Color3.fromRGB(80,255,180))
end)

eCancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() closeEditor() end)

-- ═══════ REFRESH COMMANDS LIST ═══════
local function countCommandsInTemplate(tpl, baseText)
    local str = tpl ~= "" and tpl or baseText
    local count = 0
    for _ in str:gmatch("/%w+") do count = count + 1 end
    return math.max(count, 1)
end

function refreshCommandsList()
    for _, c in ipairs(cmdList:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end
    end
    for i, cmd in ipairs(customCommands) do
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", cmdList)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 60)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = theme.card
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.LayoutOrder = i
        row.Active = false
        row.ZIndex = 14
        Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        reg(row, "card")

        local idx = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        idx.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 20)
        idx.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 6)
        idx.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        idx.Text = i .. "."
        idx.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 140, 160)
        idx.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        idx.TextSize = 11
        idx.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        idx.ZIndex = 15

        local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        txt.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 20)
        txt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 6)
        txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        txt.Text = cmd.text
        txt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        txt.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        txt.TextSize = 13
        txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        txt.ZIndex = 15

        local tmplPrev = "(no template)"
        if cmd.template and cmd.template ~= "" then
            local cnt = countCommandsInTemplate(cmd.template, cmd.text)
            tmplPrev = "📝 " .. cnt .. " → " .. cmd.template
        end
        local tmpl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        tmpl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 16)
        tmpl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 28)
        tmpl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tmpl.Text = tmplPrev
        tmpl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 140, 160)
        tmpl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        tmpl.TextSize = 9
        tmpl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        tmpl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        tmpl.ZIndex = 15

        local repBadge = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        repBadge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 20)
        repBadge.Position = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0.5, -10)
        repBadge.BackgroundColor3 = theme.primary
        repBadge.BorderSizePixel = 0
        repBadge.Active = false
        repBadge.ZIndex = 15
        Instance.new("UICorner", repBadge).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        reg(repBadge, "primary")

        local repLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", repBadge)
        repLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        repLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        repLbl.Text = "x" .. tostring(cmd.repeatCount)
        repLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        repLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        repLbl.TextSize = 10
        repLbl.ZIndex = 16

        local editBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        editBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 26)
        editBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0.5, -13)
        editBtn.BackgroundColor3 = theme.primaryDeep
        editBtn.Text = "Ed"
        editBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        editBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        editBtn.TextSize = 10
        editBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        editBtn.Active = true
        editBtn.ZIndex = 16
        Instance.new("UICorner", editBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        reg(editBtn, "primaryDeep")

        local delBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        delBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
        delBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0.5, -13)
        delBtn.BackgroundColor3 = theme.minus
        delBtn.Text = "X"
        delBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        delBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        delBtn.TextSize = 11
        delBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        delBtn.Active = true
        delBtn.ZIndex = 16
        Instance.new("UICorner", delBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        reg(delBtn, "minus")

        editBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() openEditor(i) end)
        delBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            table.remove(customCommands, i)
            saveCommands(customCommands)
            refreshCommandsList()
            setStatusLater("🗑 حذف", Color3.fromRGB(255,160,60))
        end)
    end

    if #customCommands == 0 then
        local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel", cmdList)
        empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        empty.Text = "لا توجد اوامر — اضف امر جديد"
        empty.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 140, 160)
        empty.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        empty.TextSize = 11
        empty.LayoutOrder = 1
        empty.ZIndex = 14
    end
end

addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local txt = newCmdInput.Text
    if txt == "" then return end
    for _, existing in ipairs(customCommands) do
        if existing.text == txt then return end
    end
    table.insert(customCommands, { text = txt, repeatCount = newCmdDefaultRepeat, template = "" })
    saveCommands(customCommands)
    newCmdInput.Text = ""
    refreshCommandsList()
    setStatusLater("✅ اضيف", Color3.fromRGB(80,255,180))
end)

            }
            applyThemeToAll()
        end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end)

-- ==================================================
-- ================ SPAM LOGIC =====================
-- ==================================================
local spamThread = nil

local function stopSpam()
    isSpamming = false
    if spamThread then pcall(function() task.cancel(spamThread) end); spamThread = nil end
    setStatus("جاهز", Color3.fromRGB(80,255,180))
    spamBtn.Text = "💥  سبام"
end

local function startSpam(text)
    if isSpamming then stopSpam() end
    if text == "" then setStatus("اكتب نص", theme.primaryBright); return false end
    local expanded = expandCustomCommands(text)
    isSpamming = true
    local speedText = speedMultiplier <= 0 and "⚡MAX" or ("x" .. tostring(speedMultiplier))
    setStatus("ارسال (" .. speedText .. ")", Color3.fromRGB(255,160,60))
    spamBtn.Text = "ايقاف"
    spamThread = task.spawn(function()
        while isSpamming do
            sendCommand(expanded)
            local d = getDelay()
            if d > 0 then task.wait(d) end
        end
    end)
    return true
end

spamBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if isSpamming then stopSpam() else startSpam(spamInput.Text) end
end)

genBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local target = targetInput.Text
    if target == "" then setStatus("اكتب اسم", theme.primaryBright); return end
    if #customCommands == 0 then setStatus("لا اوامر", theme.primaryBright); return end
    local expanded = {}
    for _, cmd in ipairs(customCommands) do
        local actual
        if cmd.template and cmd.template ~= "" then
            actual = cmd.template:gsub("{target}", target)
        else
            actual = cmd.text .. " " .. target
        end
        local rep = cmd.repeatCount or newCmdDefaultRepeat
        for _ = 1, rep do table.insert(expanded, actual) end
    end
    spamInput.Text = table.concat(expanded, " ")
    setStatus("ولد " .. tostring(#expanded), Color3.fromRGB(80,255,180))
    task.delay(2, function()
        if not isSpamming then setStatus("جاهز", Color3.fromRGB(80,255,180)) end
    end)
end)

    waterRunning = false
    for _, d in ipairs(activeDrops) do pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
    screenGui:Destroy()
end)

-- ==================================================
-- ============ DRAGGING ===========================
-- ==================================================
local function makeDraggable(handle, frame)
    local dragging = false
    local dragStart, startPos
    handle.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        dragging = true
        dragStart = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        startPos = frame.Position
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local current = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            local delta = current - dragStart
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)
end

makeDraggable(header, main)
makeDraggable(setHeader, settingsPanel)

local function universalClick(x, y)

    end

        pcall(function() mouse1click(x, y) end); return "mouse1click"

    if fluxus and fluxus.mouse1click then
        pcall(function() fluxus.mouse1click(x, y) end); return "Fluxus"
    end
    if delta and delta.mouse1click then
        pcall(function() delta.mouse1click(x, y) end); return "Delta"
    end
    pcall(function()
        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, true, game, 1)
        task.wait(0.005)
        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, false, game, 1)
    end)
    return "VIM"
end

    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then

            local newY = math.clamp(startY + deltaY, 70, math.max(70, viewportY - 120))

        end

local function makeCard(order, icon, title, subtitle, height)
    local Card = Instance.new("Frame", Scroll)
    Card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height or 128)
    Card.BackgroundColor3 = C_BLACK
    Card.BackgroundTransparency = 0        -- ✅ أسود نقي
    Card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Card.LayoutOrder = order
    Card.ZIndex = 4
    Instance.new("UICorner", Card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)

    local cs = Instance.new("UIStroke", Card)
    cs.Color = C_WHITE
    cs.Thickness = 1.5
    cs.Transparency = 0

    -- شريط جانبي أبيض
    local SideBar = Instance.new("Frame", Card)
    SideBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 40)
    SideBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 14)
    SideBar.BackgroundColor3 = C_WHITE
    SideBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    SideBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    SideBar.ZIndex = 6
    Instance.new("UICorner", SideBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    -- أيقونة
    local IconBox = Instance.new("Frame", Card)
    IconBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 38)
    IconBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
    IconBox.BackgroundColor3 = C_BLACK
    IconBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    IconBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    IconBox.ZIndex = 6
    Instance.new("UICorner", IconBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local iS = Instance.new("UIStroke", IconBox)
    iS.Color = C_WHITE
    iS.Thickness = 1.5
    iS.Transparency = 0

    local IconLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", IconBox)
    IconLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    IconLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    IconLbl.Text = icon
    IconLbl.TextSize = 19
    IconLbl.ZIndex = 7

    -- العنوان
    local TitleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", Card)
    TitleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 18)
    TitleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 14)
    TitleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TitleLbl.Text = title
    TitleLbl.TextColor3 = C_WHITE
    TitleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    TitleLbl.TextSize = 13
    TitleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    TitleLbl.ZIndex = 6

    local titleLine = Instance.new("Frame", Card)
    titleLine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 1.5)
    titleLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 35)
    titleLine.BackgroundColor3 = C_WHITE
    titleLine.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    titleLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    titleLine.ZIndex = 6

    local SubLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", Card)
    SubLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 12)
    SubLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 40)
    SubLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SubLbl.Text = subtitle
    SubLbl.TextColor3 = C_WHITE
    SubLbl.TextTransparency = 0.4
    SubLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    SubLbl.TextSize = 9
    SubLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    SubLbl.ZIndex = 6

    local div = Instance.new("Frame", Card)
    div.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 1)
    div.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 62)
    div.BackgroundColor3 = C_WHITE
    div.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
    div.BorderSizePixel = 0
    div.ZIndex = 6

    return Card
end

local function makeButton(parent, text, pos, size)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
    btn.Size = size
    btn.Position = pos
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = C_BLACK
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0        -- ✅ أسود نقي
    btn.Text = text
    btn.TextColor3 = C_WHITE
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    btn.TextSize = 11
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.ZIndex = 7
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
    s.Color = C_WHITE
    s.Thickness = 1.5
    s.Transparency = 0
    return btn
end

        VirtualUser:CaptureController()

            task.wait(5)

local clearPointsBtn = makeButton(clickerCard, "مسح", UDim2.new(0, 248, 0, 96), UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 22))

local ClickPoints = {}

local function renumberPoints()
    for i, p in ipairs(ClickPoints) do
        local lbl = p.gui:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
        if lbl then lbl.Text = tostring(i) end
    end
    pointsLbl.Text = "النقاط: " .. #ClickPoints
end

local function createClickPoint(startX, startY)
    if #ClickPoints >= 8 then
        pointsLbl.Text = "الحد 8 نقاط!"
        task.wait(1); renumberPoints(); return
    end
    local px = startX or (math.random(150, 700))
    local py = startY or (math.random(150, 500))

    local point = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    point.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
    point.Position = UDim2.new(0, px, 0, py)
    point.BackgroundColor3 = C_WHITE
    point.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    point.AutoButtonColor = false
    point.BorderSizePixel = 0
    point.Visible = false
    point.ZIndex = 1000
    point.Parent = gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", point).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local ps = Instance.new("UIStroke", point)
    ps.Color = C_BLACK
    ps.Thickness = 2.5
    ps.Transparency = 0

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", point)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0); lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = "1"; lbl.TextColor3 = C_BLACK; lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    lbl.TextScaled = true; lbl.ZIndex = 1001

    local dragging, dragStart, startPos
    point.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = point.Position
        end
    end)
    point.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            point.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
            )
        end
    end)
    point.InputEnded:Connect(function() dragging = false end)

    point.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
        for i, p in ipairs(ClickPoints) do
            if p.gui == point then
                point:Destroy(); table.remove(ClickPoints, i)
                renumberPoints(); break
            end
        end
    end)

    table.insert(ClickPoints, {gui = point})
    renumberPoints()
    return point
end

createClickPoint(200, 200)

addPointBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() createClickPoint() end)

clearPointsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if State.clickerActive then
        State.clickerActive = false
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(ClickPoints) do p.gui:Destroy() end
    ClickPoints = {}
    renumberPoints()
    createClickPoint(200, 200)
end)

local lastMethod = "غير معروف"

local function performClickAt(point)
    local absPos = point.AbsolutePosition
    local absSize = point.AbsoluteSize
    local cx = absPos.X + absSize.X / 2
    local cy = absPos.Y + absSize.Y / 2
    lastMethod = universalClick(cx, cy)
end

local clickThread

local function startClicker()
    if State.clickerActive then return end
    if #ClickPoints == 0 then createClickPoint(200, 200) end
    State.clickerActive = true
    for _, p in ipairs(ClickPoints) do p.gui.Visible = true end
    clickerToggle.Text = "إيقاف"
    TweenService:Create(clickerToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = C_WHITE}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(clickerToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = C_BLACK}):Play()

    methodLbl.Text = "طريقة النقر: جاري الكشف..."

    clickThread = task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.3)
        while State.clickerActive do
            for _, p in ipairs(ClickPoints) do
                if State.clickerActive and p.gui and p.gui.Parent then
                    performClickAt(p.gui)
                end
            end
            methodLbl.Text = "طريقة النقر: " .. lastMethod
            task.wait(State.clickInterval)
        end
    end)
end

local function stopClicker()
    State.clickerActive = false
    for _, p in ipairs(ClickPoints) do p.gui.Visible = false end
    clickerToggle.Text = "تشغيل"
    TweenService:Create(clickerToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = C_BLACK}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(clickerToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = C_WHITE}):Play()
    methodLbl.Text = "طريقة النقر: " .. ExecutorInfo.name
    if clickThread then task.cancel(clickThread); clickThread = nil end
end

clickerToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if State.clickerActive then stopClicker() else startClicker() end
end)

plusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    State.clickInterval = math.max(State.clickMin, State.clickInterval - State.clickStep)
    speedLbl.Text = string.format("السرعة: %.2f", State.clickInterval)
end)

minusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    State.clickInterval = math.min(State.clickMax, State.clickInterval + State.clickStep)
    speedLbl.Text = string.format("السرعة: %.2f", State.clickInterval)
end)

local radMinus = makeButton(moveCard, "−", UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 98), UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 22))
local radPlus  = makeButton(moveCard, "+", UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 98), UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 22))

local moveStartCFrame, moveStartY, moveConn, moveThread

local function startAutoMove()
    if State.autoMoveActive then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    moveStartCFrame = hrp.CFrame
    moveStartY = hrp.Position.Y
    State.autoMoveActive = true
    moveToggle.Text = "إيقاف"
    TweenService:Create(moveToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = C_WHITE}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(moveToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = C_BLACK}):Play()

    moveConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not State.autoMoveActive then return end
        local c = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not c then return end
        local r = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if r and moveStartY and math.abs(r.Position.Y - moveStartY) > 10 then
            pcall(function() r.CFrame = moveStartCFrame end)
        end
    end)

    moveThread = task.spawn(function()
        while State.autoMoveActive do
            local c = LocalPlayer.Character
            if c then
                local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and moveStartCFrame then
                    local a = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                    local d = math.random(10, State.moveRadius)
                    local tx = moveStartCFrame.Position.X + math.cos(a) * d
                    local tz = moveStartCFrame.Position.Z + math.sin(a) * d
                    hum:MoveTo(Vector3.new(tx, moveStartY, tz))
                end
            end
            task.wait(State.moveSpeedDelay)
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAutoMove()
    State.autoMoveActive = false
    moveToggle.Text = "تشغيل"
    TweenService:Create(moveToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = C_BLACK}):Play()
    TweenService:Create(moveToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = C_WHITE}):Play()
    if moveConn then moveConn:Disconnect(); moveConn = nil end
    if moveThread then task.cancel(moveThread); moveThread = nil end
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    if c then
        local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local r = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hum and r then hum:MoveTo(r.Position) end
    end
end

moveToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if State.autoMoveActive then stopAutoMove() else startAutoMove() end
end)

radPlus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    State.moveRadius = math.min(200, State.moveRadius + 10)
    radiusLbl.Text = "المدى: " .. State.moveRadius
end)

radMinus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    State.moveRadius = math.max(10, State.moveRadius - 10)
    radiusLbl.Text = "المدى: " .. State.moveRadius
end)

local isOpen = false
local openInfo  = TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local closeInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)

local function openUI()
    Main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    Main.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    Main.Visible = true
    TweenService:Create(Main, openInfo, {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 450),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0.5, -225),
    }):Play()
    isOpen = true
end

local function closeUI()
    TweenService:Create(Main, closeInfo, {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
    }):Play()
    task.wait(0.22)
    Main.Visible = false; isOpen = false
end

ToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if isOpen then closeUI() else openUI() end
end)

CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if isOpen then closeUI() end
end)

local function addHover(btn)
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = C_WHITE}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = C_BLACK}):Play()
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if btn == clickerToggle and State.clickerActive then return end
        if btn == moveToggle and State.autoMoveActive then return end
        if btn == afkToggle and State.antiafkActive then return end
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = C_BLACK}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = C_WHITE}):Play()
    end)
end

-- 🎬 فتح وإغلاق الواجهة
-- ============================================
local isOpen = false

local function openPanel()
    if isOpen then return end
    isOpen = true
    MainFrame.Visible = true
    MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    TweenService:Create(MainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 320),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -130, 0.5, -160)
    }):Play()
end

local function closePanel()
    if not isOpen then return end
    isOpen = false
    TweenService:Create(MainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    }):Play()
    task.wait(0.25)
    MainFrame.Visible = false
    MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 320)
    MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -130, 0.5, -160)
end

-- الضغط على الأيقونة
IconBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if isOpen then
        closePanel()
    else
        openPanel()
    end
end)

-- زر الإغلاق
CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closePanel)

-- ============================================
-- ⚡ منطق التنفيذ
-- ============================================
local adminRemote
pcall(function()
    adminRemote = ReplicatedStorage.HDAdminHDClient.Signals.RequestCommandModification
end)

local isExecuting = false

ExecBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if isExecuting then return end
    
    local inputCmd = CmdBox.Text
    local delayTime = tonumber(SpeedBox.Text) or 0.7
    
    if inputCmd == "" then
        -- اهتزاز عند الفراغ
        local orig = CmdBox.Position
        for i = 1, 3 do
            CmdBox.Position = orig + UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
            task.wait(0.04)
            CmdBox.Position = orig - UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
            task.wait(0.04)
        end
        CmdBox.Position = orig
        StatusText.Text = "اكتب أمر أولاً!"
        task.wait(1.5)
        StatusText.Text = "READY"
        return
    end
    
    if not adminRemote then
        StatusText.Text = "فشل الاتصال!"
        task.wait(2)
        StatusText.Text = "READY"
        return
    end
    
    isExecuting = true
    ExecBtn.Text = "EXECUTING..."
    StatusText.Text = "جاري التنفيذ..."
    
    local players = Players:GetPlayers()
    local total = #players
    local success = 0
    
    for i, p in pairs(players) do
        local finalCmd = inputCmd
        
        if string.find(finalCmd, "all") then
            finalCmd = string.gsub(finalCmd, "all", p.Name)
        elseif string.find(finalCmd, "me") then
            finalCmd = string.gsub(finalCmd, "me", p.Name)
        else
            local parts = string.split(finalCmd, " ")
            local firstWord = parts[1]
            table.remove(parts, 1)
            finalCmd = firstWord .. " " .. p.Name .. " " .. table.concat(parts, " ")
        end
        
        local ok = pcall(function() adminRemote:InvokeServer(finalCmd) end)
        if ok then success = success + 1 end
        
        StatusText.Text = i .. " / " .. total .. "  |  OK: " .. success
        task.wait(delayTime)
    end
    
    StatusText.Text = "تم!  " .. success .. " / " .. total
    ExecBtn.Text = "DONE ✓"
    task.wait(2)
    
    ExecBtn.Text = "EXECUTE ALL"
    StatusText.Text = "READY"
    isExecuting = false
end)

-- ============================================
-- ⌨️ اختصار لوحة المفاتيح (Right Ctrl)
-- ============================================
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightControl then
        if isOpen then closePanel() else openPanel() end
    end
end)

local function corner(p, r)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = (type(r) == "number") and UDim.new(0, r) or r
    c.Parent = p
    return c
end
local function stroke(p, color, thick, trans)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Color = color or BORDER
    s.Thickness = thick or 1.4
    s.Transparency = trans or 0
    s.Parent = p
    return s
end
local function label(parent, text, props)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    l.TextColor3 = WHITE
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do l[k] = v end
    l.Parent = parent
    return l
end
local function tween(obj, t, props, style, dir)
    local info = TweenInfo.new(t or 0.2, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local tw = TweenService:Create(obj, info, props)
    tw:Play()
    return tw
end

end

local function makeBtn(parent, text, props)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
    b.Text = text
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextColor3 = WHITE
    b.BackgroundColor3 = ELEV
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.TextSize = 13
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do b[k] = v end
    corner(b, 12)
    stroke(b, BORDER, 1.3, 0.1)
    return b
end

        local on = (State.Mode == key)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = on and WHITE or ELEV
        b.TextColor3 = on and BLACK or WHITE
    end
end

--=============================================================
-- DETECTOR LOGIC
--=============================================================
local function collectDetectors()
    State.Detectors = {}
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("ClickDetector") then table.insert(State.Detectors, obj) end
    end
    return #State.Detectors
end

local function fireFocus()
    for _, d in ipairs(State.Detectors) do
        if d and d.Parent then task.spawn(function() pcall(fireclickdetector, d) end) end
    end
end
local function fireNear()
    if #State.Detectors == 0 then return end
    local n = math.min(10, #State.Detectors)
    for i = 1, n do
        local d = State.Detectors[i]
        if d and d.Parent then task.spawn(function() pcall(fireclickdetector, d) end) end
    end
end
local function firePick()
    if #State.Detectors == 0 then return end
    local n = math.min(15, #State.Detectors)
    for i = 1, n do
        local d = State.Detectors[math.random(1, #State.Detectors)]
        if d and d.Parent then task.spawn(function() pcall(fireclickdetector, d) end) end
    end
end

local function setStatus(t, c)
    StatusText.Text = t
    StatusText.TextColor3 = c or GREY
end

local function startSession()
    if State.Running then return end
    local n = collectDetectors()
    if n == 0 then setStatus("✕ لا يوجد عناصر", DANGER) return end
    State.Running = true
    MainBtn.Text = "■ إيقاف التشغيل"
    MainBtn.BackgroundColor3 = DANGER
    MainBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
    setStatus("● الجلسة تعمل", OK)
    State.Thread = task.spawn(function()
        while State.Running do
            if #State.Detectors > 0 then
                if State.Mode == "FOCUS" then fireFocus()
                elseif State.Mode == "NEAR" then fireNear()
                elseif State.Mode == "PICK" then firePick() end
                task.wait(State.Speed)
            else
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function stopSession()
    State.Running = false
    if State.Thread then pcall(task.cancel, State.Thread); State.Thread = nil end
    MainBtn.Text = "◉ بدء التشغيل"
    MainBtn.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
    MainBtn.TextColor3 = BLACK
    setStatus("○ في الانتظار", GREY)
end

--=============================================================
-- PANEL SHOW / HIDE
--=============================================================
local function panelShow()
    Panel.Visible = true
    Panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    Panel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    panelStroke.Transparency = 1
    panelStrokeOuter.Transparency = 1
    tween(Panel, 0.3, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 480, 0, 460), BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    tween(panelStroke, 0.3, { Transparency = 0 })
    tween(panelStrokeOuter, 0.3, { Transparency = 0.2 })
    State.PanelOpen = true
    collectDetectors()
end

local function panelHide()
    tween(Panel, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
    tween(panelStroke, 0.2, { Transparency = 1 })
    tween(panelStrokeOuter, 0.2, { Transparency = 1 })
    task.wait(0.22)
    Panel.Visible = false
    Panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 480, 0, 460)
    Panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    panelStroke.Transparency = 0
    panelStrokeOuter.Transparency = 0.2
    State.PanelOpen = false
end

local function togglePanel()
    if State.PanelOpen then panelHide() else panelShow() end
end

--=============================================================
-- PRESS HANDLER (click vs drag)
--=============================================================
local function bindPress(btn, target, onTap)
    local active, pressTime, pressPos, moved, objStart
    local DRAG_LIMIT  = 12
    local CLICK_LIMIT = 0.7

    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            active = true
            pressTime = tick()
            pressPos = input.Position
            moved = false
            objStart = target and target.Position or nil
        end
    end)

    btn.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not active then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
        and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

        local delta = input.Position - pressPos
        if delta.Magnitude > DRAG_LIMIT then
            moved = true
            if target and objStart then
                target.Position = UDim2.new(
                    objStart.X.Scale, objStart.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    objStart.Y.Scale, objStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end
        end
    end)

    btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not active then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

        local elapsed = tick() - pressTime
        if not moved and elapsed < CLICK_LIMIT and onTap then
            pcall(onTap)
        end
        active = false
    end)
end

--=============================================================
-- WIRE UP
--=============================================================
bindPress(Float, Float, togglePanel)
bindPress(Header, Panel, nil)

CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if State.PanelOpen then panelHide() end
end)

for b, key in pairs(profileEntries) do
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.Mode = key
        refreshProfile()
        collectDetectors()
        setStatus("◆ تم تغيير النمط", WHITE)
    end)
end

FasterBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if SpeedIndex < #SPEED_STEPS then SpeedIndex = SpeedIndex + 1; updateSpeed() end
end)
SlowerBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if SpeedIndex > 1 then SpeedIndex = SpeedIndex - 1; updateSpeed() end
end)
ResetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    SpeedIndex = 4; updateSpeed()
end)

MainBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if State.Running then stopSession() else startSession() end
end)

--=============================================================
-- KEYBIND FALLBACK
--=============================================================
UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift then togglePanel() end
end)

    while Float.Parent do
        pcall(collectDetectors)
        task.wait(3)
    end
end)

local function troNotify(title, text, duration)
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = title or "TRO + WAVOR",
            Text = text or "",
            Duration = duration or 3,
        })
    end)
end

local function troRunSystem(system)
    if not system or (not system.source and not system.url) then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        -- Compile/run this system as its own chunk so its locals cannot
        -- consume the main UI chunk's local-register budget.
        local compiler = loadstring
        if type(compiler) ~= "function" then
            troNotify("TRO + WAVOR", "loadstring غير متاح في هذا الـexecutor", 4)
            return
        end

        local source = system.source
        if not source and system.url then
            local okFetch, fetched = pcall(function()
                return game:HttpGet(system.url, true)
            end)
            if not okFetch or type(fetched) ~= "string" or fetched == "" then
                troNotify("❌ " .. system.name, "تعذر تحميل السكربت", 6)
                warn("[TRO + WAVOR] Script download failed")
                return
            end
            source = fetched
        end

        local okCompile, chunkOrErr = pcall(compiler, source, "@TRO_SYSTEM_" .. tostring(system.name))
        if not okCompile or type(chunkOrErr) ~= "function" then
            troNotify("❌ " .. system.name, "Compile Error: " .. tostring(chunkOrErr), 6)
            return
        end

        local okRun, runErr = pcall(chunkOrErr)
        if not okRun then
            troNotify("❌ " .. system.name, "Runtime Error: " .. tostring(runErr), 6)
            warn("[TRO + WAVOR] Script runtime failed")
            return
        end

        troNotify("✅ TRO + WAVOR", system.name .. " اشتغل", 2)
    end)
end

    -- بحث فوري: يفلتر الاسم/الوصف، ويضع علامة بيضاء واضحة على التطابق.
    local systemRows = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(content:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") and child ~= tip and child ~= quickTools then
            local nm = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if nm then
                table.insert(systemRows, child)
            end
        end
    end

    local function applySearch(query)
        query = tostring(query or ""):lower():gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")
        local visibleCount = 0
        for _, row in ipairs(systemRows) do
            local nameLabel = row:FindFirstChild("ScriptNameLabel")
            local descLabel = row:FindFirstChild("ScriptDescLabel")
            if not nameLabel then
                for _,c in ipairs(row:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("TextLabel") and c.Text ~= "" then
                        if not nameLabel then nameLabel=c end
                    end
                end
            end
            local hay = ((nameLabel and nameLabel.Text) or "") .. " " .. ((descLabel and descLabel.Text) or "")
            local found = query == "" or hay:lower():find(query,1,true) ~= nil
            row.Visible = found
            if found then visibleCount += 1 end
            local mark = row:FindFirstChild("SearchMatchMark")
            if mark then mark:Destroy() end
            if query ~= "" and found then
                local target=nameLabel
                local lowerName=(nameLabel and nameLabel.Text or ""):lower()
                local lowerDesc=(descLabel and descLabel.Text or ""):lower()
                local targetText=target and target.Text or ""
                local pos=lowerName:find(query,1,true)
                if not pos and descLabel then target=descLabel; targetText=descLabel.Text; pos=lowerDesc:find(query,1,true) end
                if target and pos then
                    local prefix=targetText:sub(1,pos-1)
                    local ok1,mw=pcall(function() return TextService:GetTextSize(query,target.TextSize,target.Font,Vector2.new(1000,100)).X end)
                    local ok2,pw=pcall(function() return TextService:GetTextSize(prefix,target.TextSize,target.Font,Vector2.new(1000,100)).X end)
                    if ok1 and ok2 then
                        local mark=Instance.new("TextLabel",target.Parent)
                        mark.Name="SearchMatchMark"
                        mark.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0)
                        local x=target.Position.X.Offset+target.AbsoluteSize.X-pw-mw-4
                        mark.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(target.Position.X.Offset,x),target.Position.Y.Offset+2)
                        mark.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(24,mw+8),math.max(20,target.TextSize+8))
                        mark.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                        mark.Text=query
                        mark.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
                        mark.Font=target.Font
                        mark.TextSize=target.TextSize
                        mark.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                        mark.ZIndex=108
                        Instance.new("UICorner",mark).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        countText.Text = tostring(visibleCount) .. " / " .. tostring(#TRO_SYSTEMS) .. " سكربت"
    end
    searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() applySearch(searchBox.Text) end)
    applySearch("")

    return page
end
