function closeAll(returnToHome)
    -- إغلاق حقيقي: لا نترك طبقة السواد ولا أي لوحة مخفية فوق الشاشة.
    openPanelRef = nil
    if homePanel then homePanel.Visible = false end
    if suggestPanel and suggestPanel.page then suggestPanel.page.Visible = false end
    if systemsPanel then systemsPanel.Visible = false end
    if freeScriptsPanel then freeScriptsPanel.Visible = false end
    if worldDim then worldDim.Visible = false end
    if sideButtonsDock then
        sideButtonsDock.Visible = true
    end
end

local function makeHomeButton3D(btn)
    if not btn or not btn:IsA("TextButton") then return end
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    local shadow = Instance.new("Frame")
    shadow.Name = "HomeButtonDepth"
    shadow.AnchorPoint = btn.AnchorPoint
    shadow.Position = UDim2.new(btn.Position.X.Scale, btn.Position.X.Offset, btn.Position.Y.Scale, btn.Position.Y.Offset + 5)
    shadow.Size = btn.Size
    shadow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    shadow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shadow.ZIndex = math.max(1, btn.ZIndex - 1)
    shadow.Parent = btn.Parent
    Instance.new("UICorner", shadow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,14)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", shadow)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    stroke.Transparency = .9
    local rest = btn.Position
    local up = UDim2.new(rest.X.Scale,rest.X.Offset,rest.Y.Scale,rest.Y.Offset-3)
    local down = UDim2.new(rest.X.Scale,rest.X.Offset,rest.Y.Scale,rest.Y.Offset+4)
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn,TweenInfo.new(.14,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{Position=up}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(shadow,TweenInfo.new(.14),{Position=UDim2.new(rest.X.Scale,rest.X.Offset,rest.Y.Scale,rest.Y.Offset+7)}):Play()
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(btn,TweenInfo.new(.14),{Position=rest}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(shadow,TweenInfo.new(.14),{Position=UDim2.new(rest.X.Scale,rest.X.Offset,rest.Y.Scale,rest.Y.Offset+5)}):Play()
    end)
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            TweenService:Create(btn,TweenInfo.new(.06),{Position=down}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(shadow,TweenInfo.new(.06),{Position=rest}):Play()
        end
    end)
    btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            TweenService:Create(btn,TweenInfo.new(.16,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{Position=up}):Play()
            TweenService:Create(shadow,TweenInfo.new(.16),{Position=UDim2.new(rest.X.Scale,rest.X.Offset,rest.Y.Scale,rest.Y.Offset+7)}):Play()
        end
    end)
end

            if busy then return end
            busy=true; run.Text="⏳  جاري..."; run.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,220,224)
            TweenService:Create(run,TweenInfo.new(.06),{Position=down}):Play()
            task.spawn(function()
                troRunSystem(system)
                task.wait(.9)
                if run.Parent then run.Text="▶  تشغيل"; run.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); TweenService:Create(run,TweenInfo.new(.15,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{Position=rest}):Play(); busy=false end
            end)
        end)
        run.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(run,TweenInfo.new(.13,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{Position=up,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(116,49)}):Play() end)
        run.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(run,TweenInfo.new(.13),{Position=rest,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(100,42)}):Play() end)
        run.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then TweenService:Create(run,TweenInfo.new(.06),{Position=down,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(96,40)}):Play() end end)
        run.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then TweenService:Create(run,TweenInfo.new(.14,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{Position=up,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(116,49)}):Play() end end)
    end
    return page
end

homePanel = createHomePanel()
-- لا تفتح أي لوحة تلقائياً: البداية تكون بالأيقونة العائمة فقط.
homePanel.Visible = false

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
--   3D BUTTON ENGINE — Mouse + Touch
--   كل زر في واجهة TRO + WAVOR يأخذ عمقاً حقيقياً:
--   ظل سفلي + لمعان علوي + رفع عند Hover + ضغط عند Touch/Click.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
local tro3DButtons = {}

local function addTRO3DButton(button)
    if not button or not button:IsA("TextButton") then return end
    if button:GetAttribute("TRO3DReady") then return end
    button:SetAttribute("TRO3DReady", true)

    local parent = button.Parent
    if not parent then return end

    -- أزرار لوحة الإعدادات (خصوصاً شبكة الألوان) تبقى مسطحة حتى لا تغطيها طبقات الظل السوداء.
    if button:IsDescendantOf(screenGui:FindFirstChild("SettingsPanel") or Instance.new("Folder")) then
        button:SetAttribute("TRO3DReady", true)
        return
    end

    -- نترك أزرار السحب/الهيدر الفارغة بدون طبقات ضخمة.
    local isHeader = (button.Text == "" and button.BackgroundTransparency >= 0.95)
    local isInvisibleUtility = (button.BackgroundTransparency >= 0.85 and button.Text == "")
    if isHeader or isInvisibleUtility then return end

    local originalSize = button.Size
    local originalPos = button.Position
    local originalZ = button.ZIndex

    local shadow = Instance.new("Frame")
    shadow.Name = "TRO3D_Shadow"
    shadow.AnchorPoint = button.AnchorPoint
    shadow.Position = UDim2.new(
        originalPos.X.Scale, originalPos.X.Offset,
        originalPos.Y.Scale, originalPos.Y.Offset + 4
    )
    shadow.Size = originalSize
    shadow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    shadow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shadow.ZIndex = math.max(0, originalZ - 1)
    shadow.Parent = parent

    local sc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    sc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    sc.Parent = shadow

    local ss = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    ss.Thickness = 1.2
    ss.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    ss.Transparency = 0.88
    ss.Parent = shadow

    local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    scale.Name = "TRO3D_Scale"
    scale.Scale = 1
    scale.Parent = button

    local topShine = Instance.new("Frame")
    topShine.Name = "TRO3D_TopShine"
    topShine.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    topShine.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 3)
    topShine.Size = UDim2.new(0.78, 0, 0, 2)
    topShine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    topShine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.48
    topShine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topShine.ZIndex = originalZ + 2
    topShine.Parent = button
    local tc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    tc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    tc.Parent = topShine

    local edge = Instance.new("Frame")
    edge.Name = "TRO3D_BottomEdge"
    edge.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    edge.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -2)
    edge.Size = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 2)
    edge.BackgroundColor3 = accentColor
    edge.BorderSizePixel = 0
    edge.ZIndex = originalZ + 2
    edge.Parent = button
    local ec = Instance.new("UICorner")
    ec.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    ec.Parent = edge
    registerColorable(edge, "bg")

    local busy = false
    local function animate(state)
        if busy then return end
        busy = true
        local targetScale, shadowY, shadowTransparency, shineTransparency
        if state == "hover" then
            targetScale, shadowY, shadowTransparency, shineTransparency = 1.045, 5, 0.04, 0.18
        elseif state == "press" then
            targetScale, shadowY, shadowTransparency, shineTransparency = 0.975, 1, 0.24, 0.35
        else
            targetScale, shadowY, shadowTransparency, shineTransparency = 1, 4, 0.12, 0.48
        end

        TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(state == "press" and 0.055 or 0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Scale = targetScale
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(shadow, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Position = UDim2.new(originalPos.X.Scale, originalPos.X.Offset, originalPos.Y.Scale, originalPos.Y.Offset + shadowY),
            BackgroundTransparency = shadowTransparency
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(topShine, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundTransparency = shineTransparency}):Play()
        task.delay(0.15, function() busy = false end)
    end

    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() animate("hover") end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() animate("rest") end)
    button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() animate("press") end)
    button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() animate("hover") end)

    -- Touch support: لا نعتمد على Hover في الجوال.
    button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            animate("press")
        end
    end)
    button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            animate("rest")
        end
    end)

    table.insert(tro3DButtons, button)
end

-- نطبّق التأثير بعد اكتمال اللوحات حتى تكون كل أزرار الواجهة الأساسية 3D.
for _, obj in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
    if obj:IsA("TextButton") then
        addTRO3DButton(obj)
    end
end

screenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
    if obj:IsA("TextButton") then
        task.defer(function()
            if obj.Parent and obj:IsDescendantOf(screenGui) then
                addTRO3DButton(obj)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

openSystems = function()
    local page = systemsPanel
    if openPanelRef == page and page.Visible then
        closeAll(false)
        return
    end

    -- أغلق أي واجهة أخرى أولاً حتى لا تظهر أزرار Home/Settings تحت لوحة السكربتات.
    if homePanel then homePanel.Visible = false end
    if suggestPanel and suggestPanel.page then suggestPanel.page.Visible = false end
    openPanelRef = page

    local w, h = getPanelSize()
    page.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    page.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    page.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
    page.Visible = true
    worldDim.Visible = false

    if sideButtonsDock then
        sideButtonsDock.Visible = false
    end

    TweenService:Create(page, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
    }):Play()
end


openFreeScripts = function()
    local page = freeScriptsPanel
    if openPanelRef == page and page.Visible then
        closeAll(false)
        return
    end

    if homePanel then homePanel.Visible = false end
    if suggestPanel and suggestPanel.page then suggestPanel.page.Visible = false end
    if systemsPanel then systemsPanel.Visible = false end
    openPanelRef = page

    local w, h = getPanelSize()
    page.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
    page.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
    page.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20,20)
    page.Visible = true
    worldDim.Visible = false

    if sideButtonsDock then sideButtonsDock.Visible = false end

    TweenService:Create(page,TweenInfo.new(.35,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w,h)
    }):Play()
end

-- حالة البداية الصارمة: لا تظهر الواجهة الرئيسية أو قائمة السكربتات إلا بعد ضغط أيقونة 📄.
if homePanel then homePanel.Visible = false end
if systemsPanel then systemsPanel.Visible = false end
if freeScriptsPanel then freeScriptsPanel.Visible = false end
if suggestPanel and suggestPanel.page then suggestPanel.page.Visible = false end
if worldDim then worldDim.Visible = false end
if sideButtonsDock then sideButtonsDock.Visible = true end
openPanelRef = nil

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
    if gp then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
        closeAll()
    end
end)

print("✦✦✦ TRO + WAVOR v16.0 — 3D UI FIXED ✦✦✦")
print("[TRO + WAVOR] Icons: 3")
